{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0126BHAzNdM4edJ76VXuv5kT","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→```markdown\n     2→# HoloCanvas — System Architecture  \n     3→*A Micro-Gallery Blockchain for Generative Artifacts*  \n     4→\n     5→**Version:** 1.0  \n     6→**Status:** Production  \n     7→**Author:** Core Engineering Team  \n     8→**Last-Updated:** 2024-05-29  \n     9→\n    10→---\n    11→\n    12→## Table of Contents\n    13→1. Executive Summary  \n    14→2. High-Level Blueprint  \n    15→3. Component Reference  \n    16→4. Event & Data Contracts  \n    17→5. Life-Cycle State Machine  \n    18→6. Consensus Engine Plug-In Model  \n    19→7. Design Patterns In-Use  \n    20→8. Fault-Tolerance & Recovery  \n    21→9. Deployment Topology  \n    22→10. Appendix (Code Snippets, CLI, Make Targets)\n    23→\n    24→---\n    25→\n    26→## 1  Executive Summary\n    27→HoloCanvas is an open, **C-based** micro-service ecosystem that orchestrates the minting, governance, and market evolution of **generative, living NFTs**. The system exposes deterministic *creative recipes* on-chain while delegating heavy media processing to off-chain worker pods. Each component is decoupled via **Kafka** event-streams and **gRPC** contracts, allowing horizontal scale-out and plug-and-play experimentation with consensus algorithms, artistic strategies, and DeFi mechanics.\n    28→\n    29→---\n    30→\n    31→## 2  High-Level Blueprint\n    32→\n    33→```text\n    34→+------------------+        Kafka        +------------------+\n    35→|  Wallet-Proxy    | <---- Topics ----> |  Gallery-Gateway |\n    36→+--------+---------+                    +----+-------------+\n    37→         |  gRPC HTTP /1.1                   |\n    38→         |                                    |  Artifact REST\n    39→         v                                    v\n    40→ +--------+---------+       +-----------------+-----------------+\n    41→ |  LedgerCore      | <---> |   Mint-Factory (Factory Pattern)  |\n    42→ +--------+---------+       +-----------------+-----------------+\n    43→         |                                     ^\n    44→         |  RocksDB (UTXO + State)             |  Event\n    45→         v                                     |\n    46→ +--------+---------+       Kafka         +----+-------------+\n    47→ |  Governance-Hall | <---- Topics ---->  |  DeFi-Garden     |\n    48→ +------------------+                    +------------------+\n    49→```\n    50→\n    51→---\n    52→\n    53→## 3  Component Reference\n    54→\n    55→### 3.1 Wallet-Proxy (Proxy Pattern)\n    56→* Hides raw blockchain mechanics from UI clients.  \n    57→* Signs and bundles transactions, batching micro-payments into rollups.  \n    58→* Rate-limits requests per API key (Leaky-Bucket, per-tenant).  \n    59→\n    60→### 3.2 Gallery-Gateway  \n    61→* API layer (OpenAPI v3) that exposes queryable GraphQL interface.  \n    62→* HTTP → gRPC translation, static file CDN for media layers.  \n    63→\n    64→### 3.3 Mint-Factory (Factory Pattern)  \n    65→* Composes shader fragments, audio grains, and metadata into an `ArtifactRecipe`.  \n    66→* Emits `ArtifactCreated` events; persists recipe to **IPFS** and CID to chain.  \n    67→\n    68→### 3.4 LedgerCore  \n    69→* Custom UTxO‐style ledger written in C with **RocksDB** backing store.  \n    70→* Pluggable Consensus Engine (see §6).  \n    71→* Provides `LedgerService` (gRPC) with the following RPCs:  \n    72→  - `SubmitTx(Tx)`  \n    73→  - `StreamBlocks(Height)`  \n    74→  - `QueryState(Key)`  \n    75→\n    76→### 3.5 Governance-Hall  \n    77→* DAO voting, quorum tracking, parameter upgrades.  \n    78→* Runs embedded **SQLite** + **Raft** for internal persistence.  \n    79→\n    80→### 3.6 DeFi-Garden  \n    81→* Yield farming, staking pools, and fractional ownership of high-value NFTs.  \n    82→* Uses **libsecp256k1** for cryptographic ops.  \n    83→\n    84→### 3.7 Oracle-Bridge  \n    85→* Bridges external data (weather, stock, sensor) into on-chain events.  \n    86→* Provides `WeatherOracleService.Report(WeatherFrame)`  \n    87→\n    88→### 3.8 Muse Services (Observer Pattern)  \n    89→* Stateless pods that watch event streams (`ArtifactLiked`, `AuctionEnded`, etc.)  \n    90→* Trigger generative `Strategy` plug-ins that mutate media layers.\n    91→\n    92→---\n    93→\n    94→## 4  Event & Data Contracts\n    95→\n    96→### 4.1 Kafka Topics\n    97→| Topic | Publisher | Subscriber(s) | Payload Avro Schema |\n    98→|-------|-----------|---------------|---------------------|\n    99→| `tx.submitted` | Wallet-Proxy | LedgerCore | `TxEnvelope` |\n   100→| `artifact.created` | Mint-Factory | Gallery-Gateway, Muse-* | `ArtifactMeta` |\n   101→| `gov.vote.cast` | Wallet-Proxy | Governance-Hall | `VoteCast` |\n   102→| `defi.stake` | Wallet-Proxy | DeFi-Garden | `StakeEvent` |\n   103→| `oracle.weather` | Oracle-Bridge | Muse-Weather | `WeatherFrame` |\n   104→\n   105→### 4.2 gRPC / Protobuf\n   106→```proto\n   107→syntax = \"proto3\";\n   108→package holo.ledger.v1;\n   109→\n   110→message Tx {\n   111→  bytes  tx_id      = 1;\n   112→  bytes  sender     = 2;\n   113→  bytes  recipient  = 3;\n   114→  uint64 amount     = 4;\n   115→  bytes  signature  = 5;\n   116→}\n   117→\n   118→service LedgerService {\n   119→  rpc SubmitTx     (Tx)       returns (Ack);\n   120→  rpc StreamBlocks (Height)   returns (stream Block);\n   121→  rpc QueryState   (StateKey) returns (StateValue);\n   122→}\n   123→```\n   124→\n   125→---\n   126→\n   127→## 5  Life-Cycle State Machine\n   128→\n   129→```mermaid\n   130→stateDiagram-v2\n   131→    [*] --> Draft\n   132→    Draft --> Curated : curator.approve()\n   133→    Curated --> Auction : schedule.auction()\n   134→    Auction --> Fractionalized : reserve.price.met()\n   135→    Fractionalized --> Staked : defi.stake()\n   136→    Staked --> Archived : life.span.expired()\n   137→```\n   138→\n   139→States are enforced inside **LedgerCore** by a compiled **deterministic automaton** defined in `state_machine.c` (see Appendix).\n   140→\n   141→---\n   142→\n   143→## 6  Consensus Engine Plug-In Model\n   144→\n   145→`LedgerCore` dynamically loads shared-object libraries at runtime:\n   146→\n   147→```c\n   148→typedef struct {\n   149→    const char  *name;\n   150→    int  (*init)(void);\n   151→    int  (*validate_block)(const block_t *);\n   152→    int  (*commit)(const block_t *);\n   153→    void (*shutdown)(void);\n   154→} consensus_vtable_t;\n   155→```\n   156→\n   157→1. `libconsensus_pos.so` – traditional Proof-of-Stake.  \n   158→2. `libconsensus_da.so`  – Delegated-Artistry (weighted by curation reputation).  \n   159→\n   160→Each plug-in must export a `consensus_vtable` symbol.\n   161→\n   162→---\n   163→\n   164→## 7  Design Patterns In-Use\n   165→* **Factory** – `Mint-Factory` building artifacts.  \n   166→* **Observer** – Muse services listening to events.  \n   167→* **Strategy** – Generation strategies loaded by Muse pods.  \n   168→* **Proxy** – Wallet-Proxy shielding clients.  \n   169→* **State Machine** – Artifact life-cycle.  \n   170→* **Event Driven** – Kafka message bus.\n   171→\n   172→---\n   173→\n   174→## 8  Fault-Tolerance & Recovery\n   175→1. **At-Least-Once** event semantics; idempotent consumers use `dedupe_key`.  \n   176→2. **Circuit Breakers** guard cross-service gRPC calls.  \n   177→3. **Checksum** of every CID before pinning to IPFS & chain.  \n   178→4. **Snapshotting** Ledger state each 1000 blocks; restores via **RocksDB checkpoint API**.  \n   179→5. **Draining Mode**: Kubernetes preStop hook flushes pending tx to `tx.submitted`.\n   180→\n   181→---\n   182→\n   183→## 9  Deployment Topology\n   184→\n   185→```text\n   186→ ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n   187→ │                  Kubernetes Cluster                    │\n   188→ │ ┌───────────────┐  ┌───────────────┐  ┌───────────────┐ │\n   189→ │ │ Wallet-Proxy  │  │ Gallery-Gate  │  │ Oracle-Bridge │ │\n   190→ │ └───────────────┘  └───────────────┘  └───────────────┘ │\n   191→ │        |^                 |^                |^          │\n   192→ │        v|                 v|                v|          │\n   193→ │  ┌───────────────┐  ┌───────────────┐  ┌───────────────┐│\n   194→ │  │ LedgerCore    │  │ Mint-Factory  │  │ Governance    ││\n   195→ │  └───────────────┘  └───────────────┘  └───────────────┘│\n   196→ │        |                                         |      │\n   197→ │        v                                         v      │\n   198→ │   ┌───────────────┐                        ┌──────────┐ │\n   199→ │   │ DeFi-Garden   │                        │   Kafka  │ │\n   200→ │   └───────────────┘                        └──────────┘ │\n   201→ └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n   202→ ```\n   203→\n   204→*Observability*: Prometheus + Grafana dashboards for per-service latency & error-rate.\n   205→\n   206→---\n   207→\n   208→## 10  Appendix\n   209→\n   210→### 10.1 `state_machine.c` (excerpt)\n   211→\n   212→```c\n   213→#include \"state_machine.h\"\n   214→\n   215→static const char *state_names[] = {\n   216→    \"DRAFT\", \"CURATED\", \"AUCTION\", \"FRACTIONALIZED\",\n   217→    \"STAKED\", \"ARCHIVED\"\n   218→};\n   219→\n   220→static transition_t transitions[] = {\n   221→    { DRAFT,         CURATED,        EVT_CURATOR_APPROVE  },\n   222→    { CURATED,       AUCTION,        EVT_SCHEDULE_AUCTION },\n   223→    { AUCTION,       FRACTIONALIZED, EVT_RESERVE_MET      },\n   224→    { FRACTIONALIZED,STAKED,         EVT_DEFI_STAKE       },\n   225→    { STAKED,        ARCHIVED,       EVT_LIFE_EXPIRE      },\n   226→};\n   227→\n   228→artifact_state_t sm_next_state(artifact_state_t s, artifact_event_t e)\n   229→{\n   230→    for (size_t i = 0; i < ARRAY_LEN(transitions); ++i) {\n   231→        if (transitions[i].from == s && transitions[i].event == e)\n   232→            return transitions[i].to;\n   233→    }\n   234→    return s; /* No-op if invalid transition */\n   235→}\n   236→```\n   237→\n   238→### 10.2 Make Targets\n   239→\n   240→```make\n   241→build: ## Build all micro-services\n   242→\t@for svc in wallet_proxy gallery_gateway ledger_core mint_factory governance_hall defi_garden oracle_bridge ; do \\\n   243→\t\t$(MAKE) -C \"$$svc\" ; \\\n   244→\tdone\n   245→\n   246→docker-build: ## Build container images\n   247→\t@./scripts/docker-build-all.sh\n   248→```\n   249→\n   250→### 10.3 CLI Quick Start\n   251→\n   252→```bash\n   253→make build\n   254→docker-compose up -d kafka postgres\n   255→./bin/ledger_core --init-db\n   256→./bin/gallery_gateway --listen :8080\n   257→```\n The wrapper adds:\n     9→ *\n    10→ *  • Sensible default configuration tuned for low-latency micro-services\n    11→ *  • Thread-safe producer / consumer handles\n    12→ *  • Centralised error handling & structured logging\n    13→ *  • Graceful shutdown hooks\n    14→ *\n    15→ * The file is implemented header-only to avoid the boiler-plate of a separate\n    16→ * compilation unit; simply include it wherever Kafka access is required.\n    17→ *\n    18→ * NOTE: link with -lrdkafka (and -lpthread on most platforms)\n    19→ */\n    20→#ifndef HC_KAFKA_H\n    21→#define HC_KAFKA_H\n    22→\n    23→/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n    24→/* System headers                                                            */\n    25→#include <errno.h>\n    26→#include <inttypes.h>\n    27→#include <pthread.h>\n    28→#include <signal.h>\n    29→#include <stdarg.h>\n    30→#include <stdbool.h>\n    31→#include <stdint.h>\n    32→#include <stdio.h>\n    33→#include <stdlib.h>\n    34→#include <string.h>\n    35→#include <time.h>\n    36→\n    37→/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n    38→/* Third-party headers                                                       */\n    39→#include <rdkafka/rdkafka.h> /* librdkafka – https://github.com/confluentinc/librdkafka */\n    40→\n    41→#ifdef __cplusplus\n    42→extern \"C\" {\n    43→#endif\n    44→\n    45→/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n    46→/* Versioning                                                                */\n    47→#define HC_KAFKA_VERSION_MAJOR 1\n    48→#define HC_KAFKA_VERSION_MINOR 0\n    49→#define HC_KAFKA_VERSION_PATCH 0\n    50→\n    51→/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n    52→/* Logging                                                                   */\n    53→#ifndef HC_KAFKA_LOG_LEVEL\n    54→#define HC_KAFKA_LOG_LEVEL LOG_INFO\n    55→#endif\n    56→\n    57→#ifndef HC_KAFKA_LOG_TAG\n    58→#define HC_KAFKA_LOG_TAG \"hc_kafka\"\n    59→#endif\n    60→\n    61→typedef enum\n    62→{\n    63→    LOG_DEBUG = 0,\n    64→    LOG_INFO,\n    65→    LOG_WARN,\n    66→    LOG_ERROR,\n    67→    LOG_FATAL\n    68→} hc_log_level_e;\n    69→\n    70→/* Simple stdio logger; applications may override by defining\n    71→ * HC_KAFKA_LOG_FN(level, fmt, ...) prior to including this header. */\n    72→#ifndef HC_KAFKA_LOG_FN\n    73→#define HC_KAFKA_LOG_FN hc_kafka_default_log\n    74→static inline void hc_kafka_default_log(hc_log_level_e level,\n    75→                                        const char *file,\n    76→                                        int line,\n    77→                                        const char *func,\n    78→                                        const char *fmt, ...)\n    79→{\n    80→    if (level < HC_KAFKA_LOG_LEVEL)\n    81→        return;\n    82→\n    83→    static const char *LEVEL_STR[] = {\"DEBUG\", \"INFO\", \"WARN\", \"ERROR\", \"FATAL\"};\n    84→\n    85→    char timebuf[64];\n    86→    struct tm tm_now;\n    87→    struct timespec ts;\n    88→    clock_gettime(CLOCK_REALTIME, &ts);\n    89→    localtime_r(&ts.tv_sec, &tm_now);\n    90→    strftime(timebuf, sizeof(timebuf), \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\", &tm_now);\n    91→\n    92→    fprintf((level >= LOG_ERROR) ? stderr : stdout,\n    93→            \"%s.%03ld [%s] %s:%d %s() : \",\n    94→            timebuf,\n    95→            ts.tv_nsec / 1000000,\n    96→            LEVEL_STR[level],\n    97→            file,\n    98→            line,\n    99→            func);\n   100→\n   101→    va_list ap;\n   102→    va_start(ap, fmt);\n   103→    vfprintf((level >= LOG_ERROR) ? stderr : stdout, fmt, ap);\n   104→    va_end(ap);\n   105→\n   106→    fputc('\\n', (level >= LOG_ERROR) ? stderr : stdout);\n   107→\n   108→    if (level == LOG_FATAL)\n   109→        abort();\n   110→}\n   111→#endif /* HC_KAFKA_LOG_FN */\n   112→\n   113→#define HC_LOG(lvl, ...) HC_KAFKA_LOG_FN((lvl), __FILE__, __LINE__, __func__, __VA_ARGS__)\n   114→\n   115→/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n   116→/* Error handling                                                            */\n   117→\n   118→typedef enum\n   119→{\n   120→    HC_OK = 0,\n   121→    HC_ERR = -1,\n   122→    HC_ERR_TIMEOUT = -2,\n   123→    HC_ERR_EOF = -3\n   124→} hc_err_e;\n   125→\n   126→#define HC_KAFKA_MAX_ERR_STR 512\n   127→\n   128→static inline const char *hc_strerror(hc_err_e err)\n   129→{\n   130→    switch (err)\n   131→    {\n   132→    case HC_OK:\n   133→        return \"OK\";\n   134→    case HC_ERR:\n   135→        return \"Generic error\";\n   136→    case HC_ERR_TIMEOUT:\n   137→        return \"Timeout\";\n   138→    case HC_ERR_EOF:\n   139→        return \"End of partition\";\n   140→    default:\n   141→        return \"Unknown error\";\n   142→    }\n   143→}\n   144→\n   145→/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n   146→/* Configuration                                                             */\n   147→\n   148→/* The main configuration object that can be shared across producer/consumer. */\n   149→typedef struct\n   150→{\n   151→    char *bootstrap_servers; /* Comma separated list of brokers */\n   152→    char *client_id;         /* Custom client id (optional)     */\n   153→} hc_kafka_conf_t;\n   154→\n   155→/* Forward declarations */\n   156→typedef struct hc_kafka_producer_s hc_kafka_producer_t;\n   157→typedef struct hc_kafka_consumer_s hc_kafka_consumer_t;\n   158→\n   159→/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n   160→/* Global state                                                              */\n   161→\n   162→static pthread_once_t g_kafka_global_init_once = PTHREAD_ONCE_INIT;\n   163→\n   164→/* Called once per process */\n   165→static void hc_kafka__rdk_init_once(void)\n   166→{\n   167→    rd_kafka_configure_events(); /* noop stub if disabled in librdkafka build */\n   168→    /* Nothing else for now */\n   169→}\n   170→\n   171→/* Ensure global initialization of librdkafka – must be called before any API. */\n   172→static inline void hc_kafka_global_init(void)\n   173→{\n   174→    (void)pthread_once(&g_kafka_global_init_once, hc_kafka__rdk_init_once);\n   175→}\n   176→\n   177→/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n   178→/* Producer                                                                  */\n   179→\n   180→struct hc_kafka_producer_s\n   181→{\n   182→    rd_kafka_t *rk;\n   183→    rd_kafka_conf_t *conf;\n   184→    char errstr[HC_KAFKA_MAX_ERR_STR];\n   185→    pthread_mutex_t lock;\n   186→};\n   187→\n   188→static inline hc_kafka_producer_t *\n   189→hc_kafka_producer_new(const hc_kafka_conf_t *cfg)\n   190→{\n   191→    hc_kafka_global_init();\n   192→\n   193→    hc_kafka_producer_t *self = calloc(1, sizeof(*self));\n   194→    if (!self)\n   195→    {\n   196→        HC_LOG(LOG_FATAL, \"Out of memory creating producer\");\n   197→        return NULL;\n   198→    }\n   199→\n   200→    self->conf = rd_kafka_conf_new();\n   201→    rd_kafka_conf_set_log_cb(self->conf, NULL); mutex_unlock(pthread_mutex_t *m)\n   305→{ pthread_mutex_unlock(m); }\n   306→static void cond_wait(pthread_cond_t *c, pthread_mutex_t *m)\n   307→{ pthread_cond_wait(c, m); }\n   308→static void cond_signal(pthread_cond_t *c)\n   309→{ pthread_cond_signal(c); }\n   310→\n   311→#endif /* _WIN32 */\n   312→\n   313→/*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   314→/*  Observer creation / destruction                                        */\n   315→/*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   316→\n   317→muse_observer_t *muse_observer_create(const muse_config_t *cfg,\n   318→                                      muse_error_t        *out_err)\n   319→{\n   320→    if (!cfg) {\n   321→        if (out_err) *out_err = MUSE_ERR_INVALID_ARG;\n   322→        return NULL;\n   323→    }\n   324→\n   325→    muse_error_t err = MUSE_OK;\n   326→    muse_observer_t *obs = muse_xmalloc(sizeof(*obs), &err);\n   327→    if (!obs) { if (out_err) *out_err = err; return NULL; }\n   328→    memset(obs, 0, sizeof(*obs));\n   329→\n   330→    /* Shallow copy user config (strings considered owned by caller) */\n   331→    obs->cfg = *cfg;\n   332→    obs->q_capacity = cfg->max_inflight ? cfg->max_inflight : 1024;\n   333→\n   334→    /* Init synchronization primitives */\n   335→    if (mutex_init(&obs->q_mutex) != 0 ||\n   336→        cond_init (&obs->q_cond ) != 0) {\n   337→        free(obs);\n   338→        if (out_err) *out_err = MUSE_ERR_THREAD;\n   339→        return NULL;\n   340→    }\n   341→\n   342→    obs->running = 0;\n   343→    if (out_err) *out_err = MUSE_OK;\n   344→    return obs;\n   345→}\n   346→\n   347→void muse_observer_destroy(muse_observer_t *obs)\n   348→{\n   349→    if (!obs)\n   350→        return;\n   351→\n   352→    /* Stop worker thread if still running */\n   353→    muse_observer_stop(obs);\n   354→\n   355→    /* Free remaining events in queue */\n   356→    mutex_lock(&obs->q_mutex);\n   357→    muse_event_node_t *node = obs->q_head;\n   358→    while (node) {\n   359→        muse_event_node_t *next = node->next;\n   360→        free(node->event.payload);\n   361→        free(node);\n   362→        node = next;\n   363→    }\n   364→    mutex_unlock(&obs->q_mutex);\n   365→\n   366→    /* Unload plugins */\n   367→    muse_plugin_t *pl = obs->plugins;\n   368→    while (pl) {\n   369→        muse_plugin_t *next = pl->next;\n   370→        if (pl->handle)\n   371→            dlclose(pl->handle);\n   372→        free(pl->name);\n   373→        free(pl);\n   374→        pl = next;\n   375→    }\n   376→\n   377→#if defined(_WIN32)\n   378→    CloseHandle(obs->q_mutex);\n   379→    CloseHandle(obs->q_cond);\n   380→#else\n   381→    pthread_mutex_destroy(&obs->q_mutex);\n   382→    pthread_cond_destroy(&obs->q_cond);\n   383→#endif\n   384→\n   385→    free(obs);\n   386→}\n   387→\n   388→/*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   389→/*  Queue helpers                                                          */\n   390→/*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   391→\n   392→static muse_error_t enqueue_event(muse_observer_t *obs,\n   393→                                  const muse_event_t *ev)\n   394→{\n   395→    if (obs->q_depth >= obs->q_capacity)\n   396→        return MUSE_ERR_QUEUE_FULL;\n   397→\n   398→    muse_event_node_t *node = (muse_event_node_t*)malloc(sizeof(*node));\n   399→    if (!node)\n   400→        return MUSE_ERR_OOM;\n   401→\n   402→    /* Deep copy event (payload dynamically allocated) */\n   403→    node->event = *ev;\n   404→    if (ev->payload && ev->payload_len) {\n   405→        node->event.payload = (char*)malloc(ev->payload_len);\n   406→        if (!node->event.payload) { free(node); return MUSE_ERR_OOM; }\n   407→        memcpy(node->event.payload, ev->payload, ev->payload_len);\n   408→    }\n   409→    node->next = NULL;\n   410→\n   411→    /* Push to queue */\n   412→    if (!obs->q_head) {\n   413→        obs->q_head = obs->q_tail = node;\n   414→    } else {\n   415→        obs->q_tail->next = node;\n   416→        obs->q_tail = node;\n   417→    }\n   418→\n   419→    obs->q_depth++;\n   420→    return MUSE_OK;\n   421→}\n   422→\n   423→static muse_event_node_t *dequeue_event(muse_observer_t *obs)\n   424→{\n   425→    muse_event_node_t *node = obs->q_head;\n   426→    if (!node) return NULL;\n   427→    obs->q_head = node->next;\n   428→    if (!obs->q_head)\n   429→        obs->q_tail = NULL;\n   430→    obs->q_depth--;\n   431→    return node;\n   432→}\n   433→\n   434→/*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   435→/*  Event dispatcher – exposed to plug-ins                                 */\n   436→/*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   437→\n   438→static muse_error_t dispatcher_subscribe(\n   439→        struct muse_event_dispatcher *dispatcher,\n   440→        void (*cb)(const muse_event_t *, void *),\n   441→        void *user_data)\n   442→{\n   443→    /* For brevity, subscription API is not implemented in this header-only\n   444→     * version; future work could forward events only to interested\n   445→     * strategies.  For now, every loaded strategy receives all events, so\n   446→     * we simply ignore subscription requests. */\n   447→    (void)dispatcher;\n   448→    (void)cb;\n   449→    (void)user_data;\n   450→    return MUSE_OK;\n   451→}\n   452→\n   453→static muse_error_t dispatcher_emit(\n   454→        struct muse_event_dispatcher *dispatcher,\n   455→        const muse_event_t *event)\n   456→{\n   457→    muse_observer_t *obs = (muse_observer_t*)dispatcher; /* aliasing */\n   458→    return muse_observer_publish_event(obs, event);\n   459→}\n   460→\n   461→/*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   462→/*  Plug-in loader                                                         */\n   463→/*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   464→\n   465→muse_error_t muse_observer_load_plugin(muse_observer_t *obs,\n   466→                                       const char      *filename)\n   467→{\n   468→    if (!obs || !filename)\n   469→        return MUSE_ERR_INVALID_ARG;\n   470→\n   471→    /* Load shared library 482→    if (!entry) {\n   483→        fprintf(stderr, \"muse: missing muse_strategy_entry in %s\\n\", filename);\n   484→        dlclose(handle);\n   485→        return MUSE_ERR_PLUGIN;\n   486→    }\n   487→\n   488→    /* Create plug-in record ----------------------------------------------*/\n   489→    muse_plugin_t *pl = (muse_plugin_t*)calloc(1, sizeof(*pl));\n   490→    if (!pl) { 518→/*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   519→/*  Observer start / stop                                                  */\n   520→/*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   521→\n   522→muse_error_t muse_observer_start(muse_observer_t *obs)\n   523→{\n   524→    if (!obs)\n   525→ 544→muse_error_t muse_observer_stop(muse_observer_t *obs)\n   545→{\n   546→    if (!obs)\n   547→        return MUSE_ERR_INVALID_ARG;\n   548→\n   549→    if (!obs->running)\n   550→        return 588→/*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   589→/*  Diagnostics                                                            */\n 603→/*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*/\n   604→/*  Worker thread                                                          */\n       const muse_event_t  *event)\n   609→{\n   610→    muse_plugin_t *pl = obs->plugins;\n   611→    while (pl) {\n   612→        if (pl->dispatcher.subscribe) {\n   613→            /* Currently we providing:\n\n**Key Features:**\n- Thread-safe producer/consumer handles with mutex protection\n- Centralised error handling with structured logging\n- Graceful shutdown hooks\n- Configurable logging with custom callbacks\n- Default tuning for low-latency micro-services:\n  - `queue.buffering.max.ms = 5ms`\n  - `linger.ms = 0` (no message batching delay)\n  - `acks = all` (strongest durability)\n\n**Public API:**\n```c\n/* Producer API */\nhc_kafka_producer_t *hc_kafka_producer_new(const hc_kafka_conf_t *cfg);\nhc_err_e hc_kafka_producer_send(hc_kafka_producer_t *self,\n                                const char *topic,\n                                const void *payload,\n                                size_t len,\n                                int32_t partition,\n                                int block_ms);\nhc_err_e hc_kafka_producer_flush(hc_kafka_producer_t *self, int timeout_ms);\nvoid hc_kafka_producer_destroy(hc_kafka_producer_t *self);\n\n/* Consumer API */\nhc_kafka_consumer_t *hc_kafka_consumer_new(const hc_kafka_conf_t *cfg,\n                                          const char *group_id,\n                                          hc_kafka_msg_cb on_msg,\n                                          void *opaque);\nhc_err_e hc_kafka_consumer_subscribe(hc_kafka_consumer_t *self,\n                                     const char *const *topics,\n                                     size_t topic_cnt);\nhc_err_e hc_kafka_consumer_poll_loop(hc_kafka_consumer_t *self,\n                                     int timeout_ms,\n                                     volatile sig_atomic_t *stop_flag);\nvoid hc_kafka_consumer_destroy(hc_kafka_consumer_t *self);\n```\n\n#### **3.2 Common Types** (`shared/common/types.h`)\n\nCanonical primitives shared across all services:\n```c\n/* Fixed-width type aliases */\ntypedef uint8_t   u8;\ntypedef uint64_t  u64;\n\n/* Cryptographic blobs */\ntypedef struct {\n    u8 bytes[16];  /* 128-bit hash */\n} `/app/project/services/governance_hall/`\n\n#### **Event Handler** (`src/event_handler.c`)\n\n**Architecture:**\n- **Thread Model:** Single consumer thread + callback dispatcher\n- **Event Format:** JSON with signature verification\n  ```json\n  {\n    \"type\": \"VOTE_CAST\",\n    \"payload\": { ... },\n    \"sig\": \"<base64-signature>\",\n    \"timestamp\": 1684852584\n  }\n  ```\n\n**Key Functions:**\n```c\nint event_handler_start(const char *brokers,\n                       const char *group_id,\n                       const char *topics_csv,\n                       const char *pubkey_path,\n                       const char *plugins_csv);\nvoid event_handler_stop(void);\n\n/* Plugin API */\nint event_handler_register(const char *event_type,\n                           event_callback_fn cb,\n                           void *user_data);\n\ntypedef int (*event_callback_fn)(const char *event_type,\n                                 const json_t *payload,\n                                 void *user_data);\n```\n\n**Event Processing Flow:**\n1. Consumer thread polls Kafka for messages (500ms timeout)\n2. Parse JSON, validate schema (`type`, `payload`, `sig`, `timestamp`)\n3. Cryptographic signature verification using OpenSSL EVP (SHA-256)\n4. Dispatch to registered callbacks (linked-list traversal)\n5. Metrics tracking (handled events, errors)\n\n**Key Features:**\n- **Signature Verification:** Canonical JSON (type+payload+timestamp) signed with EVP\n- **Plugin System:** Dynamically loads `.so` files via `dlopen()`\n- **Signal Handling:** SIGINT/SIGTERM triggers graceful shutdown\n- **Memory Management:** Free callback & plugin lists on exit\n\n#### **Public API** (`include/governance.h`)\n\n```c\n/* Lifecycle */\nhc_gov_err_t hc_gov_ctx_create(const char *kafka_brokers,\n                               const char *storage_uri,\n                               hc_gov_ctx_t **out_ctx);\nhc_gov_err_t hc_gov_ctx_shutdown(hc_gov_ctx_t *ctx, uint32_t timeout_ms);\n\n/* Proposals */\nhc_gov_err_t hc_gov_proposal_submit(hc_gov_ctx_t *ctx,\n                                    const hc_gov_prop_header_t *header,\n                                    const void *payload,\n                                    size_t payload_len,\n                                    hc_gov_proposal_t **out_prop);\n\n/* Voting */\nhc_gov_err_t hc_gov_proposal_cast_vote(hc_gov_proposal_t *prop,\n                                       uint64_t account_id,\n                                       hc_gov_vote_choice_t choice,\n                                       uint64_t weight);\n\n/* Tallying */\nhc_gov_err_t hc_gov_proposal_get_tally(const hc_gov_proposal_t *prop,\n                                       hc_gov_tally_t *out_tally);\n\n/* Observers */\nhc_gov_err_t hc_gov_ctx_subscribe_state(hc_gov_ctx_t *ctx,\n                                        hc_gov_on_state_cb cb,\n                                        void *user_data);\n```\n\n**State Machine:**\n```\nDRAFT → ACTIVE → PASSED/REJECTED/ABORTED/EXPIRED\n```\n\n**Data Structures:**\n```c\ntypedef struct {\n    uint8_t  sig[64];              /* Ed25519 signature */\n    uint32_t creator_chain_id;\n    uint64_t created_unix_ms;\n    uint64_t vote_deadline_unix_ms;\n    char     meta[512];            /* Title, description, etc. */\n} hc_gov_prop_header_t;\n\ntypedef struct {\n    uint64_t yes_weight;\n    uint64_t no_weight;\n    uint64_t abstain_weight;\n    uint64_t voters_count;\n} hc_gov_tally_t;\n```\n\n---\n\n### 5. **Service: Mint-Factory**\n\n**Purpose:** Composes shader/audio fragments into immutable artifact recipes, mints NFTs.\n\n**Location:** `/app/project/services/mint_factory/`\n\n#### **Event Handler** (`src/event_handler.c`)\n\n**Architecture:**\n- **Thread Model:** Single consumer thread with event dispatch\n- **Event Types:**\n  ```c\n  typedef enum {\n      EVT_UNKNOWN = 0,\n      EVT_MINT_REQUEST,\n      EVT_BID,\n      EVT_GOVERNANCE_VOTE,\n      EVT_ORACLE_FEED\n  } event_type_t;\n  ```\n\n**Key Functions:**\n```c\nint evh_init(void);\nvoid evh_shutdown(void);\n\n/* Weak-linked handlers (testable via override) */\n__attribute__((weak))\nint mint_engine_handle_request(const char *payload_json);\nint mint_engine_handle_bid(const char *payload_json);\nint mint_engine_handle_governance_vote(const char *payload_json);\nint mint_engine_handle_oracle_feed(const char *payload_json);\n```\n\n**Event Processing Flow:**\n1. Consumer polls Kafka (250ms timeout)\n2. Parse JSON event format:\n   ```json\n   {\n     \"id\": \"e7b84e46-8bc1-4ed4-ab04-7574c01234d2\",\n     \"type\": \"MINT_REQUEST\",\n     \"ts\": 1695150687123,\n     \"payload\": { ... }\n   }\n   ```\n3. Extract event ID, type, timestamp, payload\n4. Keep raw payload string to avoid serialization overhead\n5. Dispatch to appropriate handler\n6. Commit Kafka offset regardless of result (poison-pill prevention)\n\n**Metrics:**\n```c\nstatic struct {\n    uint64_t processed;      /* Successfully handled events */\n    uint64_t parse_errors;   /* JSON parse failures */\n    uint64_t dispatch_errors;/* Handler failures */\n} g_metrics;\n```\n\n#### **Public API** (`include/artifact_factory.h`)\n\n```c\n/* Configuration */\nfactory_cfg_t *factory_cfg_new(void);\nartifact_err_t factory_cfg_set_kafka_brokers(factory_cfg_t *cfg,\n                                             const char *brokers);\nvoid factory_cfg_free(factory_cfg_t *cfg);\n\n/* Lifecycle */\nfactory_ctx_t *factory_ctx_bootstrap(const factory_cfg_t *cfg,\n                                     artifact_err_t *out_err);\nvoid factory_ctx_shutdown(factory_ctx_t *ctx);\n\n/* Minting */\nartifact_t *artifact_mint(factory_ctx_t *ctx,\n                         const artifact_spec_t *spec,\n                         artifact_err_t *out_err);\n\n/* Reference counting */\nartifact_t *artifact_retain(artifact_t *artifact);\nvoid artifact_release(artifact_t *artifact);\n\n/* Serialization */\nartifact_pkg_t *artifact_serialize(const artifact_t *artifact,\n                                   artifact_err_t *out_err);\nvoid artifact_pkg_free(artifact_pkg_t *pkg);\n```\n\n**Artifact Lifecycle State Machine:**\n```\nDRAFT → CURATED → AUCTION → FRACTIONALIZED → STAKED → RETIRED\n```\n\n**Data Structures:**\n```c\ntypedef struct artifact_spec {\n    artist_id_t  creator_id;\n    char         uuid[37];              /* UUIDv4 */\n    char         shader_hash[128];      /* SHA-256 hex */\n    char         audio_hash[128];\n    char         metadata_hash[128];\n    uint64_t     created_at_ms;         /* Epoch ms */\n} artifact_spec_t;\n```\n\n---\n\n### 6. **Service: Muse-Observer**\n\n**Purpose:** Event-driven service that listens to Kafka for artistic triggers and executes evolutionary strategy plug-ins.\n\n**Location:** `/app/project/services/muse_observer/`\n\n#### **Event Listener** (`src/event_listener.c`)\n\n**Architecture:**\n- **Thread Model:** Single listener thread + plugin dispatch\n- **Plugin System:** Dynamically loads strategy `.so` files\n- **Callback Pattern:** Each event forwarded to all loaded strategies\n\n**Key Functions:**\n```c\nEventListener *event_listener_create(const EventListenerConfig *cfg,\n                                     MuseActionStrategy *strategy);\nint event_listener_start(EventListener *listener);\nvoid event_listener_stop(EventListener *listener);\nvoid event_listener_destroy(EventListener *listener);\n\nstatic int dispatch_event(EventListener *listener,\n                         const char *payload, size_t len);\n```\n\n**Event Processing Flow:**\n1. Configuration setup (Kafka brokers, topic, strategy)\n2. Create Kafka consumer with group ID\n3. Set rebalance callback for partition assignment\n4. Subscribe to topic(s)\n5. Poll for messages (100ms timeout)\n6. Dispatch to strategy via `strategy->on_event()`\n7. On shutdown: unsubscribe, close consumer, destroy listener\n\n**Event JSON Format:**\n```json\n{\n  \"type\": \"artifact_liked\",\n  \"payload\": { ... }\n}\n```\n\n**Strategy *raw_json;\n    const char *type;\n} MuseEvent;\n```\n\n#### **Public API** (`include/muse.h`)\n\nHeader-only implementation with optional `MUSE_OBSERVER_IMPLEMENTATION` define:\n\n```c\n/* Lifecycle *observer);\n```\n\n**Configuration:**\n```c\ntypedef struct {\n    const char *bootstrap_servers;  /* Kafka brokers */\n    const char *group_id;           /* Consumer group */\n    const char *topics;             /* Subscription topics */\n    const char *plugin_dir;         /* Strategy plugin directory */\n    uint32_t poll_timeout_ms;       /* Poll wait time */\n    uint32_t max_inflight;          /* Queue capacity */\n    int enable_tls;                 /* TLS support */\n    const char *tls_ca_path;\n    const char *tls_cert_path;\n    const char *tls_key_path;\n} muse_config_t;\n```\n\n**Plugin Contract:**\n```c\n/* Each strategy .so must export: */\nint muse_strategy_entry(muse_event_dispatcher_t *dispatcher);\n\n/* Dispatcher provides: */\ntypedef struct {\n    muse_error_t (*subscribe)(\n        struct muse_event_dispatcher *dispatcher,\n        void (*cb)(const muse_event_t *event, void *user_data),\n        void *user_data);\n    \n    muse_error_t (*emit)(\n        struct muse_event_dispatcher *dispatcher,\n        const muse_event_t *event);\n} muse_event_dispatcher_t;\n```\n\n**Strategy Plug-ins** (`src/strategies/`):\n- `popularity_evolver.c` – Evolves art based on like counts\n- `weather_evolver.c` – Mutates shaders based on weather oracle data\n\n---\n\n### 7. **Event Handling Patterns Summary**\n\n| Service | Event Format | Validation | Consumer Model | Dispatch |\n|---------|---|---|---|---|\n| **Governance-Hall** | JSON + RSA-2048 signature | EVP SHA-256 verify | Thread + callback registry | Linked-list traversal |\n| **Mint-Factory** | JSON with id/type/ts/payload | Schema validation | Thread + enum dispatch | Switch/case handlers |\n| **Muse-Observer** | JSON with type/payload | Basic schema | Thread + plugin callbacks | Strategy interface |\n\n**Common Patterns:**\n1. **Kafka Consumer:** Single thread, 100-500ms poll timeout\n2. **Event Parsing:** JSON via jansson (except Governance uses OpenSSL)\n3. **Error Handling:** Parse errors logged, offset committed anyway (idempotence)\n4. **Shutdown:** Signal handlers trigger graceful draining + cleanup\n5. **Thread Safety:** Mutexes protect shared state (callbacks, plugins, metrics)\n\n---\n\n### 8. **Key Design Principles**\n\n1. **Decoupling via Kafka:** Services communicate asynchronously through event streams\n2. **Reference Counting:** Opaque handles with retain/release for thread-safe sharing\n3. **Plugin Architecture:** Dynamic loading of strategies/callbacks via `dlopen()`\n4. **Idempotency:** Events committed even on parse failure to prevent poison pills\n5. **At-Least-Once Semantics:** Kafka auto-commit with duplicate key deduplication in flight\n6. **Graceful Shutdown:** Signal handlers + drain queues before exit\n7. **Observability:** Metrics tracking (processed, errors), structured logging\n8. **Header-Only Option:** Muse.h & hc_kafka.h can be embedded in unit tests\n\n---\n\n### 9. **File Locations - Metrics tracking (handled events, errors)\n\n**Key Features:**\n- **Signature Verification:** Canonical JSON (type+payload+timestamp) signed with EVP\n- **Plugin System:** Dynamically loads `.so` files via `dlopen()`\n- **Signal Handling:** SIGINT/SIGTERM triggers graceful shutdown\n- **Memory Management:** Free callback & plugin lists on exit\n\n#### **Public API** (`include/governance.h`)\n\n```c\n/* Lifecycle */\nhc_gov_err_t hc_gov_ctx_create(const char *kafka_brokers,\n                               const char *storage_uri,\n                               hc_gov_ctx_t **out_ctx);\nhc_gov_err_t hc_gov_ctx_shutdown(hc_gov_ctx_t *ctx, uint32_t timeout_ms);\n\n/* Proposals */\nhc_gov_err_t hc_gov_proposal_submit(hc_gov_ctx_t *ctx,\n                                    const hc_gov_prop_header_t *header,\n                                    const void *payload,\n                                    size_t payload_len,\n                                    hc_gov_proposal_t **out_prop);\n\n/* Voting */\nhc_gov_err_t hc_gov_proposal_cast_vote(hc_gov_proposal_t *prop,\n                                       uint64_t account_id,\n                                       hc_gov_vote_choice_t choice,\n                                       uint64_t weight);\n\n/* Tallying */\nhc_gov_err_t hc_gov_proposal_get_tally(const hc_gov_proposal_t *prop,\n                                       hc_gov_tally_t *out_tally);\n\n/* Observers */\nhc_gov_err_t hc_gov_ctx_subscribe_state(hc_gov_ctx_t *ctx,\n                                        hc_gov_on_state_cb cb,\n                                        void *user_data);\n```\n\n**State Machine:**\n```\nDRAFT → ACTIVE → PASSED/REJECTED/ABORTED/EXPIRED\n```\n\n**Data Structures:**\n```c\ntypedef struct {\n    uint8_t  sig[64];              /* Ed25519 signature */\n    uint32_t creator_chain_id;\n    uint64_t created_unix_ms;\n    uint64_t vote_deadline_unix_ms;\n    char     meta[512];            /* Title, description, etc. */\n} hc_gov_prop_header_t;\n\ntypedef struct {\n    uint64_t yes_weight;\n    uint64_t no_weight;\n    uint64_t abstain_weight;\n    uint64_t voters_count;\n} hc_gov_tally_t;\n```\n\n---\n\n### 5. **Service: Mint-Factory**\n\n**Purpose:** Composes shader/audio fragments into immutable artifact recipes, mints NFTs.\n\n**Location:** `/app/project/services/mint_factory/`\n\n#### **Event Handler** (`src/event_handler.c`)\n\n**Architecture:**\n- **Thread Model:** Single consumer thread with event dispatch\n- **Event Types:**\n  ```c\n  typedef enum {\n      EVT_UNKNOWN = 0,\n      EVT_MINT_REQUEST,\n      EVT_BID,\n      EVT_GOVERNANCE_VOTE,\n      EVT_ORACLE_FEED\n  } event_type_t;\n  ```\n\n**Key Functions:**\n```c\nint evh_init(void);\nvoid evh_shutdown(void);\n\n/* Weak-linked handlers (testable via override) */\n__attribute__((weak))\nint mint_engine_handle_request(const char *payload_json);\nint mint_engine_handle_bid(const char *payload_json);\nint mint_engine_handle_governance_vote(const char *payload_json);\nint mint_engine_handle_oracle_feed(const char *payload_json);\n```\n\n**Event Processing Flow:**\n1. Consumer polls Kafka (250ms timeout)\n2. Parse JSON event format:\n   ```json\n   {\n     \"id\": \"e7b84e46-8bc1-4ed4-ab04-7574c01234d2\",\n     \"type\": \"MINT_REQUEST\",\n     \"ts\": 1695150687123,\n     \"payload\": { ... }\n   }\n   ```\n3. Extract event ID, type, timestamp, payload\n4. Keep raw payload string to avoid serialization overhead\n5. Dispatch to appropriate handler\n6. Commit Kafka offset regardless of result (poison-pill prevention)\n\n**Metrics:**\n```c\nstatic struct {\n    uint64_t processed;      /* Successfully handled events */\n    uint64_t parse_errors;   /* JSON parse failures */\n    uint64_t dispatch_errors;/* Handler failures */\n} g_metrics;\n```\n\n#### **Public API** (`include/artifact_factory.h`)\n\n```c\n/* Configuration */\nfactory_cfg_t *factory_cfg_new(void);\nartifact_err_t factory_cfg_set_kafka_brokers(factory_cfg_t *cfg,\n                                             const char *brokers);\nvoid factory_cfg_free(factory_cfg_t *cfg);\n\n/* Lifecycle */\nfactory_ctx_t *factory_ctx_bootstrap(const factory_cfg_t *cfg,\n                                     artifact_err_t *out_err);\nvoid factory_ctx_shutdown(factory_ctx_t *ctx);\n\n/* Minting */\nartifact_t *artifact_mint(factory_ctx_t *ctx,\n                         const artifact_spec_t *spec,\n                         artifact_err_t *out_err);\n\n/* Reference counting */\nartifact_t *artifact_retain(artifact_t *artifact);\nvoid artifact_release(artifact_t *artifact);\n\n/* Serialization */\nartifact_pkg_t *artifact_serialize(const artifact_t *artifact,\n                                   artifact_err_t *out_err);\nvoid artifact_pkg_free(artifact_pkg_t *pkg);\n```\n\n**Artifact Lifecycle State Machine:**\n```\nDRAFT → CURATED → AUCTION → FRACTIONALIZED → STAKED → RETIRED\n```\n\n**Data Structures:**\n```c\ntypedef struct artifact_spec {\n    artist_id_t  creator_id;\n    char         uuid[37];              /* UUIDv4 */\n    char         shader_hash[128];      /* SHA-256 hex */\n    char         audio_hash[128];\n    char         metadata_hash[128];\n    uint64_t     created_at_ms;         /* Epoch ms */\n} artifact_spec_t;\n```\n\n---\n\n### 6. **Service: Muse-Observer**\n\n**Purpose:** Event-driven service that listens to Kafka for artistic triggers and executes evolutionary strategy plug-ins.\n\n**Location:** `/app/project/services/muse_observer/`\n\n#### **Event Listener** (`src/event_listener.c`)\n\n**Architecture:**\n- **Thread Model:** Single listener thread + plugin dispatch\n- **Plugin System:** Dynamically loads strategy `.so` files\n- **Callback Pattern:** Each event forwarded to all loaded strategies\n\n**Key Functions:**\n```c\nEventListener *event_listener_create(const EventListenerConfig *cfg,\n                                     MuseActionStrategy *strategy);\nint event_listener_start(EventListener *listener);\nvoid event_listener_stop(EventListener *listener);\nvoid event_listener_destroy(EventListener *listener);\n\nstatic int dispatch_event(EventListener *listener,\n                         const char *payload, size_t len);\n```\n\n**Event Processing Flow:**\n1. Configuration setup (Kafka brokers, topic, strategy)\n2. Create Kafka consumer with group ID\n3. Set rebalance callback for partition assignment\n4. Subscribe to topic(s)\n5. Poll for messages (100ms timeout)\n6. Dispatch to strategy via `strategy->on_event()`\n7. On shutdown: unsubscribe, close consumer, destroy listener\n\n**Event JSON Format:**\n```json\n{\n  \"type\": \"artifact_liked\",\n  \"payload\": { ... }\n}\n```\n\n**Strategy Interface:**\n```c\ntypedef struct {\n    int (*on_event)(struct MuseActionStrategy *self,\n                   const MuseEvent *event);\n} MuseActionStrategy;\n\ntypedef struct {\n    json_t *raw_json;\n    const char *type;\n} MuseEvent;\n```\n\n#### **Public API** (`include/muse.h`)\n\nHeader-only implementation with optional `MUSE_OBSERVER_IMPLEMENTATION` define:\n\n```c\n/* Lifecycle */\nmuse_observer_t *muse_observer_create(const muse_config_t *cfg,\n                                      muse_error_t *out_err);\nvoid muse_observer_destroy(muse_observer_t *observer);\nmuse_error_t *observer,\n                                        const muse_event_t *event);\n\n/* Plugin management */\nmuse_error_t muse_observer_load_plugin(muse_observer_t *observer,\n                                      const char *filename);\n\n/* Diagnostics */\nuint64_t muse_observer_queue_depth(const muse_observer_t *observer);\nint muse_observer_is_running(const muse_observer_t *observer);\n```\n\n**Configuration:**\n```c\ntypedef struct {\n    const char *bootstrap_servers;  /* Kafka brokers */\n    const char *group_id;           /* Consumer group */\n    const char *topics;             /* Subscription topics */\n    const char *plugin_dir;         /* Strategy plugin directory */\n    uint32_t poll_timeout_ms;       /* Poll wait time */\n    uint32_t max_inflight;          /* Queue capacity */\n    int enable_tls;                 /* TLS support */\n    const char *tls_ca_path;\n    const char *tls_cert_path;\n    const char *tls_key_path;\n} muse_config_t;\n```\n\n**Plugin Contract:**\n```c\n/* Each strategy .so must export: */\nint muse_strategy_entry(muse_event_dispatcher_t *dispatcher);\n\n/* Dispatcher provides: */\ntypedef struct {\n    muse_error_t (*subscribe)(\n        struct muse_event_dispatcher *dispatcher,\n        void (*cb)(const muse_event_t *event, void *user_data),\n        void *user_data);\n    \n    muse_error_t (*emit)(\n        struct muse_event_dispatcher *dispatcher,\n        const muse_event_t *event);\n} muse_event_dispatcher_t;\n```\n\n**Strategy Plug-ins** (`src/strategies/`):\n- `popularity_evolver.c` – Evolves art based on like counts\n- `weather_evolver.c` – Mutates shaders based on weather oracle data\n\n---\n\n### 7. **Event Handling Patterns Summary**\n\n| Service | Event Format | Validation | Consumer Model | Dispatch |\n|---------|---|---|---|---|\n| **Governance-Hall** | JSON + RSA-2048 signature | EVP SHA-256 verify | Thread + callback registry | Linked-list traversal |\n| **Mint-Factory** | JSON with id/type/ts/payload | Schema validation | Thread + enum dispatch | Switch/case handlers |\n| **Muse-Observer** | JSON with type/payload | Basic schema | Thread + plugin callbacks | Strategy interface |\n\n**Common Patterns:**\n1. **Kafka Consumer:** Single thread, 100-500ms poll timeout\n2. **Event Parsing:** JSON via jansson (except Governance uses OpenSSL)\n3. **Error Handling:** Parse errors logged, offset committed anyway (idempotence)\n4. **Shutdown:** Signal handlers trigger graceful draining + cleanup\n5. **Thread Safety:** Mutexes protect shared state (callbacks, plugins, metrics)\n\n---\n\n### 8. **Key Design Principles**\n\n1. **Decoupling via Kafka:** Services communicate asynchronously through event streams\n2. **Reference Counting:** Opaque handles with retain/release for thread-safe sharing\n3. **Plugin Architecture:** Dynamic loading of strategies/callbacks via `dlopen()`\n4. **Idempotency:** Events committed even on parse failure to prevent poison pills\n5. **At-Least-Once Semantics:** Kafka auto-commit with duplicate key deduplication in flight\n6. **Graceful Shutdown:** Signal handlers + drain queues before exit\n7. **Observability:** Metrics tracking (processed, errors), structured logging\n8. **Header-Only Option:** Muse.h & hc_kafka.h can be embedded in unit tests\n\n---\n\n### 9. **File Locations - `/app/project/services/muse_observer/src/event_listener.c` (343 lines)\n\n**Public APIs:**\n- `/app/project/services/governance_hall/include/governance.h` (314 lines)\n- size_t len, event_t *out_evt)\n{\n    bool ok = false;\n    json_error_t jerr;\n    json_t *root = json_loadb(json_str, len, 0, &jerr);\n    if (!root) {\n        log_debug(\"JSON parse error: %s (at line %d col %d)\", jerr.text, jerr.line, jerr.column);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    /* id */\n    const char *id = json_string_value(json_object_get(root, \"id\"));\n    if (!id || strlen(id) json_string_value(json_object_get(root, \"type\"));\n    if (!type_str) {\n        log_debug(\"Event.type missing\");\n        goto cleanup;\n    }\n\n    if      (strcmp(type_str, \"MINT_REQUEST\")     == 0) out_evt->type = EVT_MINT_REQUEST;\n    else if (strcmp(type_str, \"BID\")              == 0) out_evt->type = EVT_BID;\n    else if (strcmp(type_str, \"GOVERNANCE_VOTE\")  == 0) out_evt->type = EVT_GOVERNANCE_VOTE;\n    else if (strcmp(type_str, \"ORACLE_FEED\")      == 0) out_evt->type = EVT_ORACLE_FEED;\n    else out_evt->type = EVT_UNKNOWN;\n\n    /* timestamp */\n    json_t *ts_node = (uint64_t)json_integer_value(ts_node);\n\n    /* payload (keep raw to avoid extra serialise-deserialise cycles) */\n    json_t *payload_node = json_object_get(root, \"payload\");\n    if (!payload_node) {\n        log_debug(\"Event.payload missing\");\n        goto cleanup;\n    }\n    /* Dump just the payload subtree for downstream modules */\n    out_evt->raw_payload = n, \".%03\" PRIu64 \"Z\", epoch_ms % 1000ULL);\n    return buf;\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── *\n * Signal Handling Convenience\n * ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\nstatic void sig_handler(int sig)\n{\n    (void)sig;\n    log_info(\"Received termination signal, shutting down\");\n    evh_shutdown();\n    /* Exit entire process once clean-up completes */\n    _exit(EXIT_SUCCESS);\n}\n\n/*\n * evh_install_signal_handlers – Utility function that applications may call.\n */\nvoid evh_install_signal_handlers(void)\n{\n    struct sigaction sa = {\n        .sa_handler = sig_handler,\n        .sa_flags   = 0\n    };\n    sigemptyset(&sa.sa_mask);\n    sigaction(SIGINT,  &sa, NULL);\n    sigaction(SIGTERM, &sa, NULL);\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── *\n * Unit-Test Hooks (weak linkage, allow overrides)\n * ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\n__attribute__((weak))\nint mint_engine_handle_request(const char *payload_json) {\n    (void)payload_json; return 0;\n}\n\n__attribute__((weak))\nint mint_engine_handle_bid(const char *payload_json) {\n    (void)payload_json; return 0;\n}\n\n__attribute__((weak))\nint mint_engine_handle_governance_vote(const char *payload_json) {\n    (void)payload_json; return 0;\n}\n\n__attribute__((weak))\nint mint_engine_handle_oracle_feed(const char *payload_json) {\n    (void)payload_json; return 0;\n}\n\n/* End of file */","numLines":431,"startLine":1,"totalLines":431}}}

-------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\ntypedef enum {\n    PROPOSAL_DRAFT = 0,\n    PROPOSAL_VOTING,\n    PROPOSAL_ACCEPTED,\n    PROPOSAL_REJECTED,\n    PROPOSAL_EXECUTED\n} proposal_state_t;\n\ntypedef struct governance_proposal {\n    char id[64];                /* unique sha-256/uuid string */\n    proposal_state_t state;\n    uint64_t yes_votes;\n    uint64_t no_votes;\n    time_t created_at;\n    time_t voting_deadline;     /* epoch seconds */\n    UT_hash_handle hh;          /* makes this struct hashable */\n} governance_proposal_t;\n\n/* PTHREAD_RWLOCK_INITIALIZER;\n\n/* Lookup or create a proposal record */\nstatic governance_proposal_t *\nproposal_get_or_create(const char *id)\n{\n    governance_proposal_t *p = NULL;\n    pthread_rwlock_rdlock(&g_prop_lock);\n    HASH_FIND_STR(g_proposals, id, p);\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&g_prop_lock);\n\n    if (p) {\n        return p;\n    }\n\n    /* Not found – upgrade to write lock and allocate */\n    pthread_rwlock_wrlock(&g_prop_lock);\n    HASH_FIND_STR(g_proposals, id, p);\n    if (!p) {\n        p = calloc(1, sizeof *p);\n        if (!p) {\n            pthread_rwlock_unlock(&g_prop_lock);\n            LOGE(\"Memory allocation failed for proposal '%s'\", id);\n            return NULL;\n        }\n        strncpy(p->id, id, sizeof p->id - 1);\n        p->created_at = time(NULL);\n        p->state      = PROPOSAL_DRAFT;\n        HASH_ADD_STR(g_proposals, id, p);\n        LOGI(\"Created new proposal %s\", id);\n    }\n    pthread_rwlock_unlock(&g_prop_lock);\n    return p;\n}\n\n/* Apply proposal finite-state transitions */\nstatic void proposal_try_finalize(governance_proposal_t *p)\n{\n    if (p->state != PROPOSAL_VOTING) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    time_t now = time(NULL);\n    if (now < p->voting_deadline) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    /* Time is up – tally */\n    p->state = (p->yes_votes > p->no_votes)\n                 ? PROPOSAL_ACCEPTED\n                 : PROPOSAL_REJECTED;\n\n    LOGI(\"Proposal %s finalized as %s (yes=%\" PRIu64 \", no=%\" PRIu64 \")\",\n         p->id,\n         (p->state == PROPOSAL_ACCEPTED) ? \"ACCEPTED\" : \"REJECTED\",\n         p->yes_votes, p->no_votes);\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                       Mock Event Deserialization Layer                     */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/*\n * In production we ingest binary protobuf payloads from Kafka.  For purposes of\n * this sample we accept a line-oriented JSON subset coming from stdin, Kafka,\n * or a test file.  Each line is one event:\n *\n *  { \"type\":\"PROPOSAL_CREATE\", \"id\":\"abc\", \"voting_deadline\":1660000000 }\n *  { \"type\":\"VOTE_CAST\", \"id\":\"abc\", \"vote\":\"yes\" }\n *\n * This rudimentary parser keeps the demo self-contained without pulling in a\n * heavyweight JSON stack.  It is NOT robust and must be replaced for prod.\n */\n\ntypedef enum {\n    EVT_NONE = 0,\n    EVT_PROPOSAL_CREATE,\n    EVT_VOTE_CAST,\n    EVT_FINALIZE_REQ\n} event_type_t;\n\ntypedef struct {\n != s)\n        memmove(s, p, strlen(p) + 1);\n}\n\n/* Extremely naive key search */\nstatic const char *json_get_value(const char *json, const char *key, char *buf, size_t buf_sz)\n{\n    char pattern[64];\n    snprintf(pattern, sizeof pattern, \"\\\"%s\\\":\\\"\", key);\n    const char *p = strstr(json, pattern);\n    if (!p) return NULL;\n    p += strlen(pattern);\n    const char *q = strchr(p, '\"');\n    if (!q) return NULL;\n    size_t len = (size_t)(q - p);\n    if (len >= buf_sz) len = buf_sz - 1;\n    memcpy(buf, p, len);\n    buf[len] = '\\0';\n    return buf;\n}\n\nstatic bool json_get_uint64(const char *json, const char *key, uint64_t *out)\n{\n    char pattern[64];\n    snprintf(pattern, sizeof pattern, \"\\\"%s\\\":\", key);\n    const char *p = strstr(json, pattern);\n    if (!p) return false;\n    p += strlen(pattern);\n    *out = strtoull(p, NULL, 10);\n    return true;\n}\n\nstatic event_type_t parse_event_type(const char *json)\n{\n    char \"VOTE_CAST\")       == 0) return EVT_VOTE_CAST;\n    if (strcmp(type, \"FINALIZE_REQ\")    == 0) return EVT_FINALIZE_REQ;\n    return EVT_NONE;\n}\n\nstatic bool parse_event(const char *line, event_t *out_evt)\n{\n    memset(out_evt, 0, sizeof *out_evt);\n    out_evt->type = parse_event_type(line);\n    if (out_evt->type == EVT_NONE) {\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    char id[64];\n    if -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nstatic void handle_event(const event_t *ev)\n{\n    governance_proposal_t *p = NULL;\n\n    switch (ev->type) {\n    case EVT_PROPOSAL_CREATE:\n        p = proposal_get_or_create(ev->id);\n        if (!p) break;\n\n        p->state           = PROPOSAL_VOTING;\n        p->voting_deadline = On each relevant event the observer selects a matching\n *   “strategy plug-in” (shared library placed in plugin_dir) which mutates\n *   the NFT’s media/metadata.  The resulting “evolution recipe” is forwarded\n *   to the Mint-Factory service through gRPC for on-chain execution.\n *\n * Build:\n *   cc -std=c11 -Wall -Wextra -Werror \\\n *      -o muse_observer main.c -lrdkafka -ldl -ljansson -lpthread\n *\n *   (grpc is stubbed out here; link with -lgrpc++ when a real client stub is\n *    available.)\n *\n * Copyright:\n *   © 2024 HoloCanvas Contributors.  Licensed under the Apache-2.0 license.\n */\n\n#define _GNU_SOURCE\n#include <errno.h>\n#include <getopt.h>\n#include <jansson.h>\n#include <rdkafka/rdkafka.h>\n#include <signal.h>\n#include                                                      */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nstatic volatile sig_atomic_t g_terminate = 0;\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\ntypedef struct {\n    char *kafka_brokers;\n    char *topic;\n    char *plugin_dir;\n    char *grpc_target;\n    int */\n/* Signal Handling                                                           */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nstatic void muse_on_signal(int signo) {\n    (void)signo;\n    g_terminate = 1;\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* Utility                                                                   */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nstatic char *dup_or_default(const char *src, const char *def) {\n    return strdup(src && *src ? src : def);\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* Command-line / Environment Parsing                                        */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nstatic void muse_parse_cli(int argc, char **argv, muse_cfg_t *cfg) {\n    static struct option long_opts[] = {\n        { required_argument, 0, 'g' },\n        { \"verbose\",     no_argument,       0, 'v' },\n        { \"version\",     no_argument,       0, 'V' },\n        { \"help\",        no_argument,       0, 'h' },\n        { 0,             0,                 0,  0  }\n    };\n\n    int opt;\n    while ((opt = getopt_long(argc, argv, \"b:t:p:g:vVh\", long_opts, NULL)) != -1) {\n        switch (opt) {\n        case 'b': free(cfg->kafka_brokers); cfg->kafka_brokers = strdup(optarg); break;\n        case 't': free(cfg->topic);         cfg->topic        = strdup(optarg);  break;\n        case 'p': free(cfg->plugin_dir);    cfg->plugin_dir   = strdup(optarg);  break;\n        case 'g': free(cfg->grpc_target);   cfg->grpc_target  = strdup(optarg);  break;\n        case 'v': cfg->verbosity++;                                           break;\n        case 'V':\n            printf(\"Muse Observer v%s\\n\", MUSE_VERSION);\n            exit(EXIT_SUCCESS);\n        case 'h':\n        default:\n            printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\"\n                   \"  -b, --brokers    Kafka bootstrap servers (default: %s)\\n\"\n                   \"  -t, --topic      Kafka topic to consume   (default:            */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nstatic int plugin_open(plugin_t *plg, const char *plugin_dir,\n                       const char *strategy_name)\n{\n    snprintf(plg->name, sizeof(plg->name), \"%s\", strategy_name);\n\n    char *so_path;\n    if (asprintf(&so_path, \"%s/lib%s.so\", plugin_dir, strategy_name) < 0)\n        return -1;\n\n    plg->dl_handle = dlopen(so_path, RTLD_NOW | RTLD_LOCAL);\n    free(so_path);\n\n    if (!plg->dl_handle) {\n        LOG(LOG_ERR, \"dlopen failed for strategy `%s`: %s\",\n            strategy_name, dlerror());\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    /* mandatory */\n    plg->run = (strategy_fn)dlsym(plg->dl_handle, \"muse_strategy_run\");\n    if (!plg->run) {\n        LOG(LOG_ERR, \"Plug-in `%s` missing muse_strategy_run symbol\", strategy_name);\n        dlclose(plg->dl_handle);\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    /* optional init/fini */\n    strategy_init_fn init_fn = LOG(LOG_INFO, \"Loaded strategy `%s`\", strategy_name);\n    return 0;\n}\n\nstatic void plugin_close(plugin_t *plg) {\n    if (!plg || !plg->dl_handle) return;\n    if (plg->fini) plg->fini();\n                 */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nstatic int grpc_send_evolution(const char *grpc_target,\n                               const char In a production build this would use\n     * generated gRPC C bindings to call something like\n     *   MintFactory::EvolveNFT(EvolutionRecipe)\n     */\n    (void)grpc_target; /* suppress unused warning */\n    LOG(LOG_INFO, \"Stub gRPC call sent to Mint-Factory: %s\", recipe_json);\n    return 0;\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* Kafka Consumer                                                            */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nstatic rd_kafka_t *kafka_init_consumer(const muse_cfg_t *cfg)\n{\n    char errstr[512];\n\n    rd_kafka_conf_t *conf = rd_kafka_conf_new();\n    if (rd_kafka_conf_set(conf, \"bootstrap.servers\",\n                          cfg->kafka_brokers, errstr, sizeof(errstr)) !=\n        RD_KAFKA_CONF_OK)\n        DIE(\"Kafka conf set failed: %s\", errstr);\n\n    /* Auto commit offsets every second */\n    if (rd_kafka_conf_set(conf, \"enable.auto.commit\",\n                          \"true\", errstr, sizeof(errstr)) != RD_KAFKA_CONF_OK)\n        DIE(\"Kafka conf set failed: %s\", errstr);\n\n    rd_kafka_t *rk = rd_kafka_new(RD_KAFKA_CONSUMER, conf,\n                                  errstr, sizeof(errstr));\n    if (!rk)\n        DIE(\"Failed to create Kafka consumer: %s\", errstr);\n\n    rd_kafka_poll_set_consumer(rk);\n\n    rd_kafka_topic_partition_list_t *topics =\n        rd_kafka_topic_partition_list_new(1);\n    rd_kafka_topic_partition_list_add(topics, cfg->topic, -1);\n\n    if (rd_kafka_subscribe(rk, topics))\n        DIE(\"Failed to subscribe to %s\", cfg->topic);\n\n    rd_kafka_topic_partition_list_destroy(topics);\n    LOG(LOG_INFO, \"Kafka consumer ready; brokers=%s, topic=%s\",\n        cfg->kafka_brokers, cfg->topic);\n    return rk;\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* Event Dispatcher                                                          */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nstatic int dispatch_event(const muse_cfg_t *cfg,\n                          const char       *event_json,\n                          plugin_t         *cache)\n{\n    /* ---------------------------------\n     * Strategy selection heuristics:\n     *   Input JSON must contain key\n     *     \"strategy\": \"rainbow_shift\"\n     * --------------------------------- */\n    json_error_t jerr;\n    json_t *root = json_loads(event_json, 0, &jerr);\n    if (!root) {\n        LOG(LOG_WARNING, \"Invalid JSON payload: %s\", jerr.text);\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    const char *strategy = json_string_value(json_object_get(root, \"strategy\"));\n    if (!strategy) {\n        LOG(LOG_INFO, \"No strategy specified; ignoring event\");\n        json_decref(root);\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    /* Caching: if last loaded plugin matches, reuse it */\n    plugin_t plg  = {0};\n    plugin_t *use = &plg;\n\n    if (cache && strcmp(cache->name, strategy) == 0 && cache->dl_handle) {\n        use = cache;\n    } else {\n        /* unload previous */\n        if (cache && cache->dl_handle)\n            plugin_close(cache);\n\n        /* load new */\n        if (plugin_open(&plg, cfg->plugin_dir, strategy) != 0) {\n            json_decref(root);\n            return -1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /* Call strategy */\n    char *recipe_json = NULL;\n    int rc = use->run(event_json, &recipe_json);\n    if (rc != 0 || !recipe_json) {\n        LOG(LOG_ERR, \"Strategy `%s` failed (rc=%d)\", strategy, rc);\n        json_decref(root);\n        if (use == &plg) plugin_close(&plg);\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    /* Send evolution to Mint-Factory */\n    rc = grpc_send_evolution(cfg->grpc_target, recipe_json);\n    free(recipe_json);\n\n    /* Cache plugin for reuse */\n    if (use == &plg && rc == 0 && cache) {\n        *cache = plg; /* shallow copy of handles */\n    } else if (use == &plg) {\n        plugin_close(&plg);\n    }\n\n    json_decref(root);\n    return rc;\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* Main Event Loop                                                           */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nstatic int muse_event_loop(const muse_cfg_t *cfg)\n{\n    rd_kafka_t *consumer = kafka_init_consumer(cfg);\n    int exit_code = EXIT_SUCCESS;\n    plugin_t cache = {0}; /* last-used plugin cache */\n\n    while (!g_terminate) {\n        rd_kafka_message_t *rkmsg = rd_kafka_consumer_poll(consumer,\n                                                           POLL_TIMEOUT_MS);\n        if (!rkmsg)\n            continue; /* timeout */\n\n        if (rkmsg->err) {\n            if (rkmsg->err == RD_KAFKA_RESP_ERR__PARTITION_EOF) {\n                /* not a real error */\n            } else {\n                LOG(LOG_ERR, \"Kafka error: %s\", rd_kafka_message_errstr(rkmsg));\n            }\n            rd_kafka_message_destroy(rkmsg);\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        /* The payload is not zero-terminated.  Allocate +1 char. */\n        char *payload = malloc(rkmsg->len + 1);\n        if (!payload) DIE(\"malloc failed\");\n        memcpy(payload, rkmsg->payload, rkmsg->len);\n        payload[rkmsg->len] = '\\0';\n\n        if (dispatch_event(cfg, payload, &cache) != 0) {\n            LOG(LOG_WARNING, \"Event processing failed\");\n            /* keep running */\n        }\n\n        free(payload);\n        rd_kafka_message_destroy(rkmsg);\n    }\n\n    LOG(LOG_INFO, \"Shutting down…\");\n\n    /* Graceful teardown */\n    if (cache.dl_handle)\n        plugin_close(&cache);\n\n    rd_kafka_consumer_close(consumer);\n    rd_kafka_destroy(consumer);\n    rd_kafka_wait_destroyed(2000);\n\n    return exit_code;\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* Entry Point                                                               */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nint main(int argc, char **argv)\n{\n    openlog(\"holo-muse\", LOG_PID | LOG_CONS, LOG_DAEMON);\n\n    muse_cfg_t cfg = {\n        .kafka_brokers = dup_or_default(getenv(\"MUSE_KAFKA_BROKERS\"),\n                                        DEFAULT_BROKERS),\n        .topic         = dup_or_default(getenv(\"MUSE_TOPIC\"),      DEFAULT_TOPIC),\n        .plugin_dir    = dup_or_default(getenv(\"MUSE_PLUGIN_DIR\"), DEFAULT_PLUGDIR),\n        .grpc_target   = dup_or_default(getenv(\"MUSE_GRPC_TARGET\"),DEFAULT_GRPC_TGT),\n        .verbosity     = 0\n    };\n\n    muse_parse_cli(argc, argv, &cfg);\n\n    /* Adjust syslog verbosity */\n    setlogmask(LOG_UPTO(cfg.verbosity ? LOG_DEBUG : LOG_INFO));\n\n    LOG(LOG_INFO, \"Muse Observer v%s starting…\", MUSE_VERSION);\n\n    /* Install signals */\n    struct sigaction sa = {\n        .sa_handler = muse_on_signal,\n        .sa_flags   = 0\n    };\n    sigemptyset(&sa.sa_mask);\n    sigaction(SIGINT,  &sa, NULL);\n    sigaction(SIGTERM, &sa, NULL);\n\n    int rc = muse_event_loop(&cfg);\n\n    free(cfg.kafka_brokers);\n    free(cfg.topic);\n    free(cfg.plugin_dir);\n    free(cfg.grpc_target);\n\n    closelog();\n    return rc;\n}\n```","numLines":420,"startLine":1,"totalLines":420}}}

It provides:\n     8→ *\n     9→ *   • A strictly-typed, 32-bit error code layout that unifies error handling\n    10→ *     across service boundaries.\n    11→ *   • Sub-system identifiers (Cryptograph, LedgerCore, etc.) so that any error\n    12→ *     can be traced back to its originating bounded context.\n    13→ *   • Severity levels that allow transport-agnostic log routing (stderr,\n    14→ *     syslog, Kafka topic, OpenTelemetry, …).\n    15→ *   • Convenience macros for building, de-constructing and propagating errors\n    16→ *     with zero boilerplate.\n    17→ *\n    18→ * Copyright (c) 2023-2024 The HoloCanvas contributors\n    19→ * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0\n    20→ */\n    21→\n    22→#ifndef HOLOCANVAS_SHARED_COMMON_ERRORS_H\n    23→#define HOLOCANVAS_SHARED_COMMON_ERRORS_H\n    24→\n    25→/*───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────*/\n    26→#include <stdint.h>\n    27→#include <stdio.h>\n    28→\n    29→#ifdef __cplusplus\n    30→extern \"C\" {\n    31→#endif\n    32→/*───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────*/\n    33→\n    34→/**\n    35→ * 32-bit error code layout\n    36→ *\n    37→ *   31 ── 24 │ Sub-system  (8 bits)\n    38→ *   23 ── 20 │ Severity    (4 bits)\n    39→ *   19 ── 16 │ Reserved    (4 bits)\n    40→ *   15 ──  0 │ Detail      (16 bits)\n    41→ */\n    42→typedef uint32_t hc_error_t;\n    43→\n    44→/*===========================================================================*/\n    45→/* Sub-system identifiers (8 bits)                                           */\n    46→/*===========================================================================*/\n    47→typedef enum {\n    48→    HC_SUBSYS_UNDEFINED     = 0x00,\n    49→    HC_SUBSYS_CRYPTOGRAPH   = 0x01,\n    50→    HC_SUBSYS_LEDGER_CORE   = 0x02,\n    51→    HC_SUBSYS_MINT_FACTORY  = 0x03,\n    52→    HC_SUBSYS_GALLERY_GATE  = 0x04,\n    53→    HC_SUBSYS_DEFI_GARDEN   = 0x05,\n    54→    HC_SUBSYS_ORACLE_BRIDGE = 0x06,\n    55→    HC_SUBSYS_WALLET_PROXY  = 0x07,\n    56→    HC_SUBSYS_GOVERNANCE    = 0x08,\n    57→    HC_SUBSYS_COMMON        = 0xFE,   /* Utility & shared libs  */\n    58→    HC_SUBSYS_UNKNOWN       = 0xFF\n    59→} hc_subsys_t;\n    60→\n    61→/*===========================================================================*/\n    62→/* Severity levels (4 bits)                                                  */\n    63→/*===========================================================================*/\n    64→typedef enum {\n    65→    HC_SEV_SUCCESS  = 0x0,   /* Non-error, success                      */\n    66→    HC_SEV_INFO     = 0x1,   /* Informative message, no action required */\n    67→    HC_SEV_WARNING  = 0x2,   /* Recoverable error                       */\n    68→    HC_SEV_ERROR    = 0x3,   /* Non-fatal error                         */\n    69→    HC_SEV_FATAL    = 0x4    /* Unrecoverable, must abort               */\n    70→} hc_sev_t;\n    71→\n    72→/*===========================================================================*/\n    73→/* Macro helpers                                                             */\n    74→/*===========================================================================*/\n    75→#define HC_ERROR_PACK(subsys, sev, detail) \\\n    76→    ((((uint32_t)(subsys)  & 0xFFu) << 24) | \\\n    77→     (((uint32_t)(sev)     & 0x0Fu) << 20) | \\\n    78→     (((uint32_t)(detail)  & 0xFFFFu)))\n    79→\n    80→#define HC_ERROR_SUBSYS(code)   ((hc_subsys_t)(((code) >> 24) & 0xFFu))\n    81→#define HC_ERROR_SEVERITY(code) ((hc_sev_t)   (((code) >> 20) & 0x0Fu))\n    82→#define HC_ERROR_DETAIL(code)   ((uint16_t)   ((code) & 0xFFFFu))\n    83→\n    84→#define HC_OK  ((hc_error_t)0)  /* Convenience alias for success */\n    85→\n    86→/*===========================================================================*/\n    87→/* Common detail codes (16 bits)                                             */\n    88→/* Service-specific headers should extend their own detail ranges starting   */\n    89→/* at 0x0100 to avoid overlap with these commons.                            */\n    90→/*===========================================================================*/\n    91→typedef enum {\n    92→    HC_EC_SUCCESS             = 0x0000,\n    93→    HC_EC_INVALID_ARGUMENT    = 0x0001,\n    94→    HC_EC_OUT_OF_MEMORY       = 0x0002,\n    95→    HC_EC_IO_ERROR            = 0x0003,\n    96→    HC_EC_TIMEOUT             = 0x0004,\n    97→    HC_EC_NOT_IMPLEMENTED     = 0x0005,\n    98→    HC_EC_DATA_CORRUPT        = 0x0006,\n    99→    HC_EC_PERMISSION_DENIED   = 0x0007,\n   100→    HC_EC_STATE_VIOLATION     = 0x0008,\n   101→    HC_EC_DEPENDENCY_FAILURE  = 0x0009,\n   102→    HC_EC_NETWORK_UNREACHABLE = 0x000A,\n   103→    HC_EC_ASSERTION_FAILED    = 0x000B,\n   104→    HC_EC_UNKNOWN             = 0xFFFF\n   105→} hc_ec_t;\n   106→\n   107→/*===========================================================================*/\n   108→/* Public API                                                                */\n   109→/*===========================================================================*/\n   110→\n   111→/**\n   112→ * Convert an error code into a human-readable, short message.\n   113→ *\n   114→ * Implemented inline for header-only availability, but backed by an internal\n   115→ * constant table to keep it lightweight. The wrapper adds:\n *\n *  • Sensible default configuration tuned for low-latency micro-services\n *  • Thread-safe producer / consumer handles\n *  • Centralised error handling & structured logging\n *  • Graceful shutdown hooks\n *\n * The file is implemented header-only to avoid the boiler-plate of a separate\n * compilation unit; simply include it wherever Kafka access is required.\n *\n * NOTE: link with -lrdkafka (and -lpthread on most platforms)\n */\n#ifndef HC_KAFKA_H\n#define HC_KAFKA_H\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* System headers                                                            */\n#include <errno.h>\n#include <inttypes.h>\n#include <pthread.h>\n#include <signal.h>\n#include <stdarg.h>\n#include <stdbool.h>\n#include <stdint.h>\n#include <stdio.h>\n#include <stdlib.h>\n#include <string.h>\n#include <time.h>\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* Third-party headers                                                       */\n#include <rdkafka/rdkafka.h> /* librdkafka – https://github.com/confluentinc/librdkafka */\n\n#ifdef __cplusplus\nextern \"C\" {\n#endif\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* Versioning                                                                */\n#define HC_KAFKA_VERSION_MAJOR 1\n#define HC_KAFKA_VERSION_MINOR 0\n#define HC_KAFKA_VERSION_PATCH 0\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* Logging                                                                   */\n#ifndef HC_KAFKA_LOG_LEVEL\n#define HC_KAFKA_LOG_LEVEL LOG_INFO\n#endif\n\n#ifndef HC_KAFKA_LOG_TAG\n#define HC_KAFKA_LOG_TAG \"hc_kafka\"\n#endif\n\ntypedef enum\n{\n    LOG_DEBUG = 0,\n    LOG_INFO,\n    LOG_WARN,\n    LOG_ERROR,\n    LOG_FATAL\n} hc_log_level_e;\n\n/* Simple stdio logger; applications may override by defining\n * HC_KAFKA_LOG_FN(level, fmt, ...) prior to including this header. */\n#ifndef HC_KAFKA_LOG_FN\n#define HC_KAFKA_LOG_FN hc_kafka_default_log\nstatic inline void hc_kafka_default_log(hc_log_level_e level,\n                                        const char *file,\n                                        int line,\n                                        const char *func,\n                                        const char *fmt, ...)\n{\n    if (level < HC_KAFKA_LOG_LEVEL)\n        return;\n\n    static const char *LEVEL_STR[] = {\"DEBUG\", \"INFO\", \"WARN\", \"ERROR\", \"FATAL\"};\n\n    char timebuf[64];\n    struct tm tm_now;\n    struct timespec ts;\n    clock_gettime(CLOCK_REALTIME, &ts);\n    localtime_r(&ts.tv_sec, &tm_now);\n    strftime(timebuf, sizeof(timebuf), \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\", &tm_now);\n\n    fprintf((level >= LOG_ERROR) ? stderr : stdout,\n            \"%s.%03ld [%s] %s:%d %s() : \",\n            timebuf,\n            ts.tv_nsec / 1000000,\n            LEVEL_STR[level],\n            file,\n            line,\n            func);\n\n    va_list ap;\n    va_start(ap, fmt);\n    vfprintf((level >= LOG_ERROR) ? stderr : stdout, fmt, ap);\n    va_end(ap);\n\n    fputc('\\n', (level >= LOG_ERROR) ? stderr : stdout);\n\n    if (level == LOG_FATAL)\n        abort();\n}\n#endif /* HC_KAFKA_LOG_FN */\n\n#define HC_LOG(lvl, ...) HC_KAFKA_LOG_FN((lvl), __FILE__, __LINE__, __func__, __VA_ARGS__)\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* Error handling                                                            */\n\ntypedef enum\n{\n    HC_OK = 0,\n    HC_ERR = -1,\n    HC_ERR_TIMEOUT = -2,\n    HC_ERR_EOF = -3\n} hc_err_e;\n\n#define HC_KAFKA_MAX_ERR_STR 512\n\nstatic inline const char *hc_strerror(hc_err_e err)\n{\n    switch (err)\n    {\n    case HC_OK:\n        return \"OK\";\n    case HC_ERR:\n        return \"Generic error\";\n    case HC_ERR_TIMEOUT:\n        return \"Timeout\";\n    case HC_ERR_EOF:\n        return \"End of partition\";\n    default:\n        return \"Unknown error\";\n    }\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* Configuration                                                             */\n\n/* The main configuration object that can be shared across producer/consumer. */\ntypedef struct\n{\n    char *bootstrap_servers;
