/* Use default internal logging */\n\n    if (rd_kafka_conf_set(self->conf, \"bootstrap.servers\", cfg->bootstrap_servers,\n                          self->errstr, sizeof(self->errstr)) != RD_KAFKA_CONF_OK)\n    {\n        HC_LOG(LOG_ERROR, \"Kafka conf error: %s\", self->errstr);\n        goto fail;\n    }\n\n    if (cfg->client_id &&\n        rd_kafka_conf_set(self->conf, \"client.id\", cfg->client_id,\n                          self->errstr, sizeof(self->errstr)) != RD_KAFKA_CONF_OK)\n    {\n        HC_LOG(LOG_WARN, \"Kafka conf client.id: %s\", self->errstr);\n    }\n\n    /* Improve latency for micro-services */\n    rd_kafka_conf_set(self->conf, \"queue.buffering.max.ms\", \"5\", NULL, 0);\n    rd_kafka_conf_set(self->conf, \"linger.ms\", \"0\", NULL, 0);\n    rd_kafka_conf_set(self->conf, \"acks\", \"all\", NULL, 0);\n\n    self->rk = rd_kafka_new(RD_KAFKA_PRODUCER, self->conf,\n                            self->errstr, sizeof(self->errstr));\n    if (!self->rk)\n    {\n        HC_LOG(LOG_ERROR, \"Kafka producer init: %s\", self->errstr);\n        goto fail;\n    }\n\n    pthread_mutex_init(&self->lock, NULL);\n    HC_LOG(LOG_INFO, \"Kafka producer created (client.id=%s)\",\n           cfg->client_id ? cfg->client_id : \"N/A\");\n    return self;\n\nfail:\n    if (self->conf)\n        rd_kafka_conf_destroy(self->conf);\n    free(self);\n    return NULL;\n}\n\n/*\n * Non-blocking send – copies payload, returns immediately.\n * On success returns HC_OK.  In case of queue‐full backpressure, waits\n * (block_ms) milliseconds before giving up with HC_ERR_TIMEOUT.\n */\nstatic inline hc_err_e\nhc_kafka_producer_send(hc_kafka_producer_t *self,\n                       const char *topic,\n                       const void *payload,\n                       size_t len,\n                       int32_t partition, /* RD_KAFKA_PARTITION_UA for any */\n                       int block_ms)\n{\n    if (!self || !topic)\n        return HC_ERR;\n\n    rd_kafka_resp_err_t err;\n    int remaining_wait = block_ms;\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&self->lock);\n\nretry:\n    err = rd_kafka_producev(\n        self->rk,\n        RD_KAFKA_V_TOPIC(topic),\n        RD_KAFKA_V_PARTITION(partition),\n        RD_KAFKA_V_MSGFLAGS(RD_KAFKA_MSG_F_COPY),\n        RD_KAFKA_V_VALUE(payload, len),\n        RD_KAFKA_V_END);\n\n    if (!err)\n    {\n        pthread_mutex_unlock(&self->lock);\n        return HC_OK; /* queued successfully */\n    }\n\n    if (err == RD_KAFKA_RESP_ERR__QUEUE_FULL && remaining_wait > 0)\n    {\n        rd_kafka_poll(self->rk, 0);  /* serve delivery reports */\n        const int backoff = 50;      /* ms */\n        struct timespec ts = {.tv_sec = 0, .tv_nsec = backoff * 1000000};\n        nanosleep(&ts, NULL);\n        remaining_wait -= backoff;\n        goto retry;\n    }\n\n    HC_LOG(LOG_ERROR, \"Produce failed: %s\", rd_kafka_err2str(err));\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&self->lock);\n    return HC_ERR;\n}\n\n/* Poll delivery callbacks.  Return number of events served. */\nstatic inline int\nhc_kafka_producer_poll(hc_kafka_producer_t *self, int timeout_ms)\n{\n    return rd_kafka_poll(self->rk, timeout_ms);\n}\n\n/* Flush outstanding messages; timeout_ms < 0 waits indefinitely. */\nstatic inline hc_err_e\nhc_kafka_producer_flush(hc_kafka_producer_t *self, int timeout_ms)\n{\n    int ret = rd_kafka_flush(self->rk, timeout_ms < 0 ? INT_MAX : timeout_ms);\n    return (ret == RD_KAFKA_RESP_ERR_NO_ERROR) ? HC_OK : HC_ERR_TIMEOUT;\n}\n\nstatic inline void\nhc_kafka_producer_destroy(hc_kafka_producer_t *self)\n{\n    if (!self)\n        return;\n\n    hc_kafka_producer_flush(self, 10 * 1000);\n    rd_kafka_destroy(self->rk);\n    pthread_mutex_destroy(&self->lock);\n    free(self);\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* Consumer                                                                  */\n\ntypedef void (*hc_kafka_msg_cb)(const rd_kafka_message_t *rkmsg, void *opaque);\n\nstruct hc_kafka_consumer_s\n{\n    rd_kafka_t *rk;\n    rd_kafka_conf_t *conf;\n    rd_kafka_topic_partition_list_t *subscribed;\n    hc_kafka_msg_cb on_msg;\n    void *opaque;\n    char errstr[HC_KAFKA_MAX_ERR_STR];\n};\n\nstatic inline hc_kafka_consumer_t *\nhc_kafka_consumer_new(const hc_kafka_conf_t *cfg,\n                      const char *group_id,\n                      hc_kafka_msg_cb on_msg,\n                      void *opaque)\n{\n    hc_kafka_global_init();\n\n    hc_kafka_consumer_t *self = calloc(1, sizeof(*self));\n    if (!self)\n        return NULL;\n\n    self->conf = rd_kafka_conf_new();\n    rd_kafka_conf_set(self->conf, \"bootstrap.servers\", cfg->bootstrap_servers, NULL, 0);\n    rd_kafka_conf_set(self->conf, \"group.id\", group_id, NULL, 0);\n    rd_kafka_conf_set(self->conf, \"auto.offset.reset\", \"earliest\", NULL, 0);\n    rd_kafka_conf_set(self->conf, \"enable.auto.commit\", \"false\", NULL, 0);\n    rd_kafka_conf_set(self->conf, \"enable.partition.eof\", \"true\", NULL, 0);\n\n    if (cfg->client_id)\n        rd_kafka_conf_set(self->conf, \"client.id\", cfg->client_id, NULL, 0);\n\n    self->rk = rd_kafka_new(RD_KAFKA_CONSUMER, self->conf,\n                            self->errstr, sizeof(self->errstr));\n    if (!self->rk)\n    {\n        HC_LOG(LOG_ERROR, \"Kafka consumer new: %s\", self->errstr);\n        goto fail;\n    }\n\n    rd_kafka_poll_set_consumer(self->rk); /* Redirect rd_kafka_poll() to consumer_poll */\n\n    self->on_msg = on_msg;\n    self->opaque = opaque;\n\n    HC_LOG(LOG_INFO, \"Kafka consumer created (group=%s)\", group_id);\n    return self;\n\nfail:\n    if (self->conf)\n        rd_kafka_conf_destroy(self->conf);\n    free(self);\n    return NULL;\n}\n\nstatic inline hc_err_e\nhc_kafka_consumer_subscribe(hc_kafka_consumer_t *self,\n                            const char *const *topics,\n                            size_t topic_cnt)\n{\n    if (!self || topic_cnt == 0)\n        return HC_ERR;\n\n    self->subscribed = rd_kafka_topic_partition_list_new((int)topic_cnt);\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < topic_cnt; ++i)\n        rd_kafka_topic_partition_list_add(self->subscribed, topics[i], RD_KAFKA_PARTITION_UA);\n\n    rd_kafka_resp_err_t err = rd_kafka_subscribe(self->rk, self->subscribed);\n    if (err)\n    {\n        HC_LOG(LOG_ERROR, \"Failed to subscribe: %s\", rd_kafka_err2str(err));\n        rd_kafka_topic_partition_list_destroy(self->subscribed);\n        self->subscribed = NULL;\n        return HC_ERR;\n    }\n    return HC_OK;\n}\n\n/* Blocking poll loop helper; returns HC_ERR on fatal error or when stop flag set */\nstatic inline hc_err_e\nhc_kafka_consumer_poll_loop(hc_kafka_consumer_t *self,\n                            int timeout_ms,\n                            volatile sig_atomic_t *stop_flag)\n{\n    while (!(*stop_flag))\n    {\n        rd_kafka_message_t *rkmsg = rd_kafka_consumer_poll(self->rk, timeout_ms);\n        if (!rkmsg)\n            continue;\n\n        if (rkmsg->err)\n        {\n            if (rkmsg->err == RD_KAFKA_RESP_ERR__PARTITION_EOF)\n            {\n                /* Silently ignore EOFs to avoid log spam */\n                rd_kafka_message_destroy(rkmsg);\n                continue;\n            }\n            HC_LOG(LOG_WARN, \"Consumer error: %s\", rd_kafka_message_errstr(rkmsg));\n            rd_kafka_message_destroy(rkmsg);\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        /* Dispatch to user callback */\n        if (self->on_msg)\n            self->on_msg(rkmsg, self->opaque);\n\n        rd_kafka_message_destroy(rkmsg);\n    }\n\n    return HC_OK;\n}\n\nstatic inline hc_err_e\nhc_kafka_consumer_commit(hc_kafka_consumer_t *self)\n{\n    rd_kafka_resp_err_t err = rd_kafka_commit(self->rk, NULL, false);\n    if (err)\n    {\n        HC_LOG(LOG_ERROR, \"Commit failed: %s\", rd_kafka_err2str(err));\n        return HC_ERR;\n    }\n    return HC_OK;\n}\n\nstatic inline void\nhc_kafka_consumer_destroy(hc_kafka_consumer_t *self)\n{\n    if (!self)\n        return;\n\n    rd_kafka_consumer_close(self->rk);\n    rd_kafka_topic_partition_list_destroy(self->subscribed);\n    rd_kafka_destroy(self->rk);\n    free(self);\n}\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/* Utilities                                                                 */\n\n/* Convert timeval to milliseconds since epoch */\nstatic inline int64_t hc_now_ms(void)\n{\n    struct timespec ts;\n    clock_gettime(CLOCK_REALTIME, &ts);\n    return (int64_t)ts.tv_sec * 1000 + ts.tv_nsec / 1000000;\n}\n\n#ifdef __cplusplus\n} /* extern \"C\" */\n#endif\n\n#endif /* HC_KAFKA_H */\n","numLines":478,"startLine":1,"totalLines":478}}}

This module allows microservices to register\n * handlers for specific event types, abstracting away the boilerplate of\n * Kafka consumer setup, JSON parsing, and event dispatching.\n *\n * Usage:\n *   1. Create a dispatcher with event_dispatcher_create()\n *   2. Register handlers for event types with event_dispatcher_register_handler()\n *   3. Start the event loop with event_dispatcher_run()\n *   4. Clean up with event_dispatcher_destroy()\n *\n * Copyright (c) 2023-2024 The HoloCanvas contributors\n * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0\n */\n\n#ifndef HOLOCANVAS_SHARED_EVENT_DISPATCHER_H\n#define HOLOCANVAS_SHARED_EVENT_DISPATCHER_H\n\n#include <stddef.h>\n#include <stdint.h>\n#include <stdbool.h>\n\n/* Include protobuf-c if available, otherwise ProtobufCMessage;\n#endif\n\n/* Include our common error types */\n#include \"common/errors.h\"\n\n#ifdef __cplusplus\nextern \"C\" {\n#endif\n\n/*===========================================================================*/\n/* Types                                                                     */\n/*===========================================================================*/\n\n/**\n * Event handler function signature.\n *\n * Handlers receive:\n *   - event_type: The string key identifying the event type\n *   - payload: The raw JSON payload string (null-terminated)\n *   - payload_len: Length of the payload string\n *   - user_context: User-provided context data passed during dispatcher creation\n *\n * Returns:\n *   HC_OK on success, or an appropriate error code on failure.\n */\ntypedef hc_error_t (*event_handler_func_t)(const char *event_type,\n                                           const char *payload,\n                                           size_t payload_len,\n                                           void *user_context);\n\n/**\n * Alternative handler signature that receives parsed protobuf message.\n * Only used if protobuf-c is available.\n */\ntypedef hc_error_t (*event_handler_proto_func_t)(const ProtobufCMessage *msg,\n                                                  void *user_context);\n\n/**\n * Opaque event dispatcher context.\n */\ntypedef struct EventDispatcher EventDispatcher;\n\n/**\n * Configuration for the event dispatcher.\n */\ntypedef struct {\n    const char *kafka_brokers;   /**< Comma-separated list of Kafka brokers */\n    const char *topic;           /**< Topic to subscribe to */\n    const char *group_id;        /**< Consumer group ID */\n    int poll_timeout_ms;         /**< Kafka poll timeout (default: 500ms) */\n    int max_handlers;            /**< Maximum number of handlers (default: 64) */\n} EventDispatcherConfig;\n\n/*===========================================================================*/\n/* Public API                                                                */\n/*===========================================================================*/\n\n/**\n * Create a new event dispatcher.\n *\n * @param kafka_brokers Comma-separated list of Kafka broker addresses\n * @param topic         The Kafka topic to subscribe to\n * @param group_id      The consumer group ID\n * @param user_context  User-provided context passed to all handlers\n *\n * @return A new EventDispatcher instance, or NULL on failure\n */\nEventDispatcher *event_dispatcher_create(const char *kafka_brokers,\n                                         const char *topic,\n                                         const char *group_id,\n                                         void *user_context);\n\n/**\n * Create a new event dispatcher with extended configuration.\n *\n * @param config       Configuration structure\n * @param user_context User-provided context passed to all handlers\n *\n * @return A new EventDispatcher instance, or NULL on failure\n */\nEventDispatcher *event_dispatcher_create_ex(const EventDispatcherConfig *config,\n                                            void *user_context);\n\n/**\n * Register an event handler for a specific event type.\n *\n * The event_type_key is matched against the \"type\" field in incoming JSON\n * messages. Multiple handlers can be registered for the same event type;\n * they will be called in registration order.\n *\n * @param dispatcher     The event dispatcher instance\n * @param event_type_key The event type string to match (e.g., \"VOTE_CAST\")\n * @param handler        The handler function to invoke for matching events\n *\n * @return HC_OK on success, error code on failure\n */\nhc_error_t event_dispatcher_register_handler(EventDispatcher *dispatcher,\n                                             const char *event_type_key,\n                                             event_handler_func_t handler);\n\n/**\n * Register a wildcard handler that receives all events.\n *\n * Wildcard handlers are called after type-specific handlers.\n *\n * @param dispatcher The event dispatcher instance\n * @param handler    The handler function to invoke for all events\n *\n * @return HC_OK on success, error code on failure\n */\nhc_error_t event_dispatcher_register_wildcard(EventDispatcher *dispatcher,\n                                              event_handler_func_t handler);\n\n/**\n * Start the event consumption and dispatch loop.\n *\n * This function blocks and continuously polls Kafka for messages, dispatching\n * them to registered handlers. It returns when:\n *   - event_dispatcher_stop() is called from another thread\n *   - A fatal error occurs\n *   - The process receives SIGINT or SIGTERM (if signal handling is enabled)\n *\n * @param dispatcher The event dispatcher instance\n */\nvoid event_dispatcher_run(EventDispatcher *dispatcher);\n\n/**\n * Signal the dispatcher to stop processing events.\n *\n * This is thread-safe and can be called from signal handlers.\n *\n * @param dispatcher The event dispatcher instance\n */\nvoid event_dispatcher_stop(EventDispatcher *dispatcher);\n\n/**\n * Check if the dispatcher is currently running.\n *\n * @param dispatcher The event dispatcher instance\n * @return true if running, false otherwise\n */\nbool event_dispatcher_is_running(const EventDispatcher *dispatcher);\n\n/**\n * Destroy the event dispatcher and free all resources.\n *\n * If the dispatcher is still running, this function will stop it first.\n *\n * @param dispatcher The event dispatcher instance to destroy\n */\nvoid event_dispatcher_destroy(EventDispatcher *dispatcher);\n\n/**\n * Get statistics from the dispatcher.\n */\ntypedef struct {\n    uint64_t events_received;\n    uint64_t events_dispatched;\n    uint64_t events_unhandled;\n    uint64_t parse_errors;\n    uint64_t handler_errors;\n} EventDispatcherStats;\n\n/**\n * Get current statistics from the dispatcher.\n *\n * @param dispatcher The event dispatcher instance\n * @param stats      Output parameter for statistics\n *\n * @return HC_OK on success\n */\nhc_error_t event_dispatcher_get_stats(const EventDispatcher *dispatcher,\n                                      EventDispatcherStats *stats);\n\n/*===========================================================================*/\n/* Convenience Macros {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-5-20251101","id":"msg_01McEUX87Dx1eeFoFfRXJARB","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01GrR8RjoYHd6QQe4Mc5f1ch","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/logs/agent/solution.md","content":"# Solution: Refactor Event Handling into Shared Event Dispatcher Module\n\n## Overview\n\nThis solution implements a major architectural refactoring by creating a centralized, generic event dispatcher module in the `shared` library. This new module encapsulates the Kafka consumption loop and event routing logic that was previously duplicated across multiple services (`governance_hall`, `mint_factory`, and `muse_observer`).\n\n## Analysis\n\n### Current State (Before Refactoring)\n\nThe three services all implement similar patterns:\n1. **governance_hall/src/event_handler.c** (481 lines): Uses a linked-list of callbacks registered via `event_handler_register()`. The `consumer_loop()` function polls Kafka, parses JSON, validates signatures, and dispatches to registered callbacks by iterating through the linked list.\n\n2. **mint_factory/src/event_handler.c** (431 lines): Uses an enum-based switch statement in `dispatch_event()` to route events. Contains `evh_init()` and `evh_shutdown()` lifecycle functions.\n\n3. **muse_observer/src/event_listener.c** (343 lines): Uses a strategy pattern where a single `MuseActionStrategy` receives all events via `dispatch_event()`.\n\n### Common Patterns Identified\n\nAll three services share:\n- Kafka consumer setup (rd_kafka_conf_set, rd_kafka_new, rd_kafka_subscribe)\n- Main consumption loop with polling\n- JSON parsing with jansson\n- Event type extraction from JSON\n- Handler/callback invocation\n- Graceful shutdown via signal handling\n- Error logging and metrics\n\n### Design Decisions\n\n1. **Hash Map for Handler Registry**: More efficient than linked-list for lookup, especially as the number of handlers grows. Uses open addressing with linear probing for simplicity.\n\n2. **String-based Event Type Keys**: Matches the JSON `\"type\"` field directly, avoiding enum conversion overhead.\n\n3. **Callback Signature**: Uses `(const char* event_type, const char* payload, size_t len, void* user_context)` to support both JSON and potential protobuf payloads.\n\n4. **Thread Safety**: Uses mutex protection for handler registration while the dispatcher is running.\n\n5. **Wildcard Handlers**: Support for handlers that receive all events (like the muse_observer pattern).\n\n---\n\n## New Files to Create\n\n### File 1: `shared/event_dispatcher/hc_event_dispatcher.h`\n\n```c\n/**\n * hc_event_dispatcher.h\n *\n * HoloCanvas - Generic Event Dispatcher Module\n * --------------------------------------------\n * A reusable, generic event dispatcher that centralizes the Kafka consumption\n * loop and event routing logic. This module allows microservices to register\n * handlers for specific event types, abstracting away the boilerplate of\n * Kafka consumer setup, JSON parsing, and event dispatching.\n *\n * Usage:\n *   1. Create a dispatcher with event_dispatcher_create()\n *   2. Register handlers for event types with event_dispatcher_register_handler()\n *   3. Start the event loop with event_dispatcher_run()\n *   4. Clean up with event_dispatcher_destroy()\n *\n * Copyright (c) 2023-2024 The HoloCanvas contributors\n * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0\n */\n\n#ifndef HOLOCANVAS_SHARED_EVENT_DISPATCHER_H\n#define HOLOCANVAS_SHARED_EVENT_DISPATCHER_H\n\n#include <stddef.h>\n#include <stdint.h>\n#include <stdbool.h>\n\n/* Include protobuf-c if available, otherwise use forward declaration */\n#ifdef PROTOBUF_C_H_INCLUDED\n#include <protobuf-c/protobuf-c.h>\n#else\n/* Forward declaration for protobuf message base type */\ntypedef struct ProtobufCMessage ProtobufCMessage;\n#endif\n\n/* Include our common error types */\n#include \"common/errors.h\"\n\n#ifdef __cplusplus\nextern \"C\" {\n#endif\n\n/*===========================================================================*/\n/* Types                                                                     */\n/*===========================================================================*/\n\n/**\n * Event handler function signature.\n *\n * Handlers receive:\n *   - event_type: The string key identifying the event type\n *   - payload: The raw JSON payload string (null-terminated)\n *   - payload_len: Length of the payload string\n *   - user_context: User-provided context data passed during dispatcher creation\n *\n * Returns:\n *   HC_OK on success, or an appropriate error code on failure.\n */\ntypedef hc_error_t (*event_handler_func_t)(const char *event_type,\n                                           const char *payload,\n                                           size_t payload_len,\n                                           void *user_context);\n\n/**\n * Alternative handler signature that receives parsed protobuf message.\n * Only used if protobuf-c is available.\n */\ntypedef hc_error_t (*event_handler_proto_func_t)(const ProtobufCMessage *msg,\n                                                  void *user_context);\n\n/**\n * Opaque event dispatcher context.\n */\ntypedef struct EventDispatcher EventDispatcher;\n\n/**\n * Configuration for the event dispatcher.\n */\ntypedef struct {\n    const char *kafka_brokers;   /**< Comma-separated list of Kafka brokers */\n    const char *topic;           /**< Topic to subscribe to */\n    const char *group_id;        /**< Consumer group ID */\n    int poll_timeout_ms;         /**< Kafka poll timeout (default: 500ms) */\n    int max_handlers;            /**< Maximum number of handlers (default: 64) */\n} EventDispatcherConfig;\n\n/*===========================================================================*/\n/* Public API                                                                */\n/*===========================================================================*/\n\n/**\n * Create a new event dispatcher.\n *\n * @param kafka_brokers Comma-separated list of Kafka broker addresses\n * @param topic         The Kafka topic to subscribe to\n * @param group_id      The consumer group ID\n * @param user_context  User-provided context passed to all handlers\n *\n * @return A new EventDispatcher instance, or NULL on failure\n */\nEventDispatcher *event_dispatcher_create(const char *kafka_brokers,\n                                         const char *topic,\n                                         const char *group_id,\n                                         void *user_context);\n\n/**\n * Create a new event dispatcher with extended configuration.\n *\n * @param `shared/event_dispatcher/hc_event_dispatcher.c`\n\n```c\n/**\n * hc_event_dispatcher.c\n *\n * HoloCanvas - Generic Event Dispatcher Implementation\n * -----------------------------------------------------\n * Implementation of the centralized event dispatcher module.\n *\n * Copyright (c) 2023-2024 The HoloCanvas contributors\n * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0\n */\n\n#define _POSIX_C_SOURCE 200809L\n\n#include \"hc_event_dispatcher.h\"\n\n#include <errno.h>\n#include <pthread.h>\n#include <signal.h>\n#include <librdkafka/rdkafka.h>\n\n/*===========================================================================*/\n/* Configuration Defaults                                                    */\n/*===========================================================================*/\n\n#define DEFAULT_POLL_TIMEOUT_MS  500\n#define DEFAULT_MAX_HANDLERS     64\n#define HANDLER_MAP_LOAD_FACTOR  0.75\n#define MAX_EVENT_TYPE_LEN       128\n\n/*===========================================================================*/\n/* Logging                                                                   */\n/*===========================================================================*/\n\n#define LOG_PREFIX \"[EventDispatcher] \"\n#define LOG_ERROR(fmt, ...) fprintf(stderr, LOG_PREFIX \"ERROR: \" fmt \"\\n\", ##__VA_ARGS__)\n#define LOG_WARN(fmt, ...)  fprintf(stderr, LOG_PREFIX \"WARN:  \" fmt \"\\n\", ##__VA_ARGS__)\n#define LOG_INFO(fmt, ...)  fprintf(stdout, LOG_PREFIX \"INFO:  \" fmt \"\\n\", ##__VA_ARGS__)\n#define LOG_DEBUG(fmt, ...) fprintf(stdout, LOG_PREFIX \"DEBUG: \" fmt \"\\n\", ##__VA_ARGS__)\n\n/*===========================================================================*/\n/* Internal Data Structures                                                  */\n/*===========================================================================*/\n\n/**\n * Hash map entry for event handlers.\n * Uses open addressing with linear probing.\n */\ntypedef struct {\n    char *event_type;               /**< Event type key (owned) */\n    event_handler_func_t handler;   /**< Handler function */\n    bool occupied;                  /**< Slot occupied flag */\n} HandlerEntry;\n\n/**\n * Linked list node for multiple handlers on same event type.\n */\ntypedef struct HandlerNode {\n    event_handler_func_t handler;\n    struct HandlerNode *next;\n} rd_kafka_topic_partition_list_t *sub_topics;\n\n    /* Configuration */\n    char *kafka_brokers;\n    char *topic;\n    char *group_id;\n    int poll_timeout_ms;\n\n    /* Handler registry (hash map atomic_uint_fast64_t stats_dispatched;\n    atomic_uint_fast64_t stats_unhandled;\n    atomic_uint_fast64_t stats_parse_errors;\n    atomic_uint_fast64_t stats_handler_errors;\n};\n\n/* Global dispatcher for signal handling */\nstatic EventDispatcher *g_signal_dispatcher = NULL;\n\n/*===========================================================================*/\n/* Hash Function                                                             */\n/*===========================================================================*/\n\n/**\n * djb2 hash function for strings.\n */\nstatic size_t hash_string(const char *str) {\n    size_t hash = 5381;\n    int c;\n    while ((c = *str++)) {\n        hash = ((hash << 5) + hash) + (size_t)c;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\n/*===========================================================================*/\n/* Handler Map Operations                                                    */\n/*===========================================================================*/\n\nstatic HandlerBucket *handler_map_find(EventDispatcher *self, const char *event_type) {\n    if (!self->handler_map || !event_type) return NULL;\n\n    size_t idx = hash_string(event_type) % self->handler_map_size;\n\n    /* Linear probing */\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < self->handler_map_size; ++i) {\n        size_t probe_idx = (idx + i) % self->handler_map_size;\n        HandlerBucket *bucket = &self->handler_map[probe_idx];\n\n        if (!bucket->event_type) {\n            return NULL; /* Empty slot - not found */\n        }\n\n        if (strcmp(bucket->event_type, event_type) == 0) {\n            return bucket;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return NULL;\n}\n\nstatic hc_error_t handler_map_insert(EventDispatcher *self,\n                                     const char *event_type,\n                                     event_handler_func_t handler) {\n    if (!self->handler_map || !event_type || !handler) {\n        return HC_ERROR_PACK(HC_SUBSYS_COMMON, HC_SEV_ERROR, HC_EC_INVALID_ARGUMENT);\n    }\n\n    /* Check load factor */\n    if ((double)self->handler_count / (double)self->handler_map_size > HANDLER_MAP_LOAD_FACTOR) {\n        LOG_WARN(\"Handler map approaching capacity limit\");\n    }\n\n    /* Find or create bucket */\n    size_t idx = hash_string(event_type) % self->handler_map_size;\n    HandlerBucket *bucket = NULL;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < self->handler_map_size; ++i) {\n        size_t probe_idx = (idx + i) % self->handler_map_size;\n        HandlerBucket *probe = &self->handler_map[probe_idx];\n\n        if (!probe->event_type) {\n            /* Empty slot - use it */\n            probe->event_type = strdup(event_type);\n            if (!probe->event_type) {\n                return HC_ERROR_PACK(HC_SUBSYS_COMMON, HC_SEV_ERROR, HC_EC_OUT_OF_MEMORY);\n            }\n            probe->handlers = NULL;\n            bucket = probe;\n            self->handler_count++;\n            break;\n        }\n\n        if (strcmp(probe->event_type, event_type) == 0) {\n            /* Existing bucket for this event type */\n            bucket = probe;\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (!bucket) {\n        LOG_ERROR(\"Handler map is full\");\n        return HC_ERROR_PACK(HC_SUBSYS_COMMON, HC_SEV_ERROR, HC_EC_OUT_OF_MEMORY);\n    }\n\n    /* Add handler to bucket's linked list */\n    HandlerNode *node = malloc(sizeof(HandlerNode));\n    if (!node) {\n        return HC_ERROR_PACK(HC_SUBSYS_COMMON, HC_SEV_ERROR, HC_EC_OUT_OF_MEMORY);\n    }\n\n    node->handler = handler;\n    node->next = bucket->handlers;\n    bucket->handlers = node;\n\n    return HC_OK;\n}\n\nstatic void handler_map_destroy(EventDispatcher *self) {\n    if (!self->handler_map) return;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < self->handler_map_size; ++i) {\n        HandlerBucket *bucket = &self->handler_map[i];\n\n        if (bucket->event_type) {\n            free(bucket->event_type);\n\n            /* Free handler nodes */\n            HandlerNode *node = bucket->handlers;\n            while (node) {\n                HandlerNode *next = node->next;\n                free(node);\n                node = next;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    free(self->handler_map);\n    self->handler_map = NULL;\n}\n\n/*===========================================================================*/\n/* Event Processing                                                          */\n/*===========================================================================*/\n\nstatic int dispatch_to_handlers(EventDispatcher *self,\n                                const char *event_type,\n                                const char *payload,\n                                size_t payload_len) {\n    int handlers_called = 0;\n    bool had_errors = false;\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&self->handler_lock);\n\n    /* Find handlers for this event type */\n    HandlerBucket *bucket = handler_map_find(self, event_type);\n    if (bucket && bucket->handlers) {\n        for (HandlerNode *node = bucket->handlers; node; node = node->next) {\n            hc_error_t err = node->handler(event_type, payload, payload_len, self->user_context);\n            handlers_called++;\n\n            if (err != HC_OK) {\n                LOG_WARN(\"Handler for '%s' returned error: %s\", event_type, hc_error_str(err));\n                had_errors = true;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /* Call wildcard handlers */\n    for (HandlerNode *node = self->wildcard_handlers; node; node = node->next) {\n        hc_error_t err = node->handler(event_type, payload, payload_len, self->user_context);\n        handlers_called++;\n\n        if (err != HC_OK) {\n            LOG_WARN(\"Wildcard handler returned error: %s\", hc_error_str(err));\n            had_errors = true;\n        }\n    }\n\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&self->handler_lock);\n\n    if (had_errors) {\n        atomic_fetch_add(&self->stats_handler_errors, 1);\n    }\n\n    return handlers_called;\n}\n\nstatic void process_message(EventDispatcher *self,\n                           const void *payload,\n                           size_t len) {\n    atomic_fetch_add(&self->stats_received, 1);\n\n    /* Parse JSON */\n    json_error_t jerr;\n    json_t *root = json_loadb((const char *)payload, len, 0, &jerr);\n    if (!root) {\n        LOG_WARN(\"JSON parse error at line %d: %s\", jerr.line, jerr.text);\n        atomic_fetch_add(&self->stats_parse_errors, 1);\n        return;\n    }\n\n    /* Extract event type */\n    json_t *type_json = json_object_get(root, \"type\");\n    if (!json_is_string(type_json)) {\n        LOG_WARN(\"Event missing 'type' field\");\n        json_decref(root);\n        atomic_fetch_add(&self->stats_parse_errors, 1);\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const char *event_type = json_string_value(type_json);\n\n    /* Extract payload (or use entire message) */\n    json_t *payload_json = json_object_get(root, \"payload\");\n    char *payload_str = NULL;\n    size_t payload_len = 0;\n\n    if (payload_json) {\n        payload_str = json_dumps(payload_json, JSON_COMPACT);\n        payload_len = payload_str ? strlen(payload_str) : 0;\n    } else {\n        /* Use entire message as payload */\n        payload_str = strndup((const char *)payload, len);\n        payload_len = len;\n    }\n\n    if (!payload_str) {\n        LOG_ERROR(\"Failed to extract payload\");\n        json_decref(root);\n        return;\n    }\n\n    /* Dispatch to handlers */\n    int handlers_called = dispatch_to_handlers(self, event_type, payload_str, payload_len);\n\n    if (handlers_called > 0) {\n        atomic_fetch_add(&self->stats_dispatched, 1);\n        LOG_DEBUG(\"Event '%s' dispatched to %d handler(s)\", event_type, handlers_called);\n    } else {\n        atomic_fetch_add(&self->stats_unhandled, 1);\n        LOG_DEBUG(\"No handlers for event type '%s'\", event_type);\n    }\n\n    free(payload_str);\n    json_decref(root);\n}\n\n/*===========================================================================*/\n/* Consumer Loop                                                             */\n/*===========================================================================*/\n\nstatic void consumer_loop(EventDispatcher *self) {\n    LOG_INFO(\"Event dispatcher consumer loop started\");\n\n    while (atomic_load(&self->running)) {\n        rd_kafka_message_t *rkmsg = rd_kafka_consumer_poll(self->rk, self->poll_timeout_ms);\n\n        if (!rkmsg) {\n            continue; /* Timeout - poll again */\n        }\n\n        if (rkmsg->err) {\n            if (rkmsg->err == RD_KAFKA_RESP_ERR__PARTITION_EOF) {\n                /* End of partition - not an error */\n                rd_kafka_message_destroy(rkmsg);\n                continue;\n            }\n            LOG_WARN(\"Kafka error: %s\", rd_kafka_message_errstr(rkmsg));\n            rd_kafka_message_destroy(rkmsg);\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        /* Process the message */\n        process_message(self, rkmsg->payload, rkmsg->len);\n\n        rd_kafka_message_destroy(rkmsg);\n    }\n\n    LOG_INFO(\"Event dispatcher consumer loop stopped\");\n}\n\n/*===========================================================================*/\n/* Signal Handling                                                           */\n/*===========================================================================*/\n\nstatic void signal_handler(int sig) {\n    (void)sig;\n    if (g_signal_dispatcher) {\n        event_dispatcher_stop(g_signal_dispatcher);\n    }\n}\n\nvoid event_dispatcher_install_signal_handlers(EventDispatcher *dispatcher) {\n    g_signal_dispatcher = dispatcher;\n\n    struct sigaction sa = {\n        .sa_handler = signal_handler,\n        .sa_flags = 0\n    };\n    sigemptyset(&sa.sa_mask);\n\n    sigaction(SIGINT, &sa, NULL);\n    sigaction(SIGTERM, &sa, NULL);\n}\n\n/*===========================================================================*/\n/* Public API Implementation                                                 */\n/*===========================================================================*/\n\nEventDispatcher *event_dispatcher_create(const char *kafka_brokers,\n *user_context) {\n    if (!config || !config->kafka_brokers || !config->topic || !config->group_id) {\n        LOG_ERROR(\"Invalid configuration\");\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    EventDispatcher }\n\n    HandlerNode *node = malloc(sizeof(HandlerNode));\n    if (!node) {\n        return HC_ERROR_PACK(HC_SUBSYS_COMMON, HC_SEV_ERROR, HC_EC_OUT_OF_MEMORY);\n    }\n\n 0;\n}\n\n/* Load strategy plugin */\nstatic int load_strategy(const char *plugin_dir, const char *strategy_name) {\n    if (g_plugin_handle && strcmp(g_current_strategy, strategy_name) == 0) {\n        return 0; /* Already loaded */\n    }\n\n    /* Unload previous */\n    if (g_plugin_handle) {\n        dlclose(g_plugin_handle);\n        g_plugin_handle = NULL;\n        g_strategy_run = NULL;\n    }\n\n    /* Load new */\n    char path[512];\n    snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"%s/lib%s.so\", plugin_dir, strategy_name);\n\n    g_plugin_handle = dlopen(path, RTLD_NOW | RTLD_LOCAL);\n    if return -1;\n    }\n\n    strncpy(g_current_strategy, strategy_name, sizeof(g_current_strategy) - 1);\n    LOG_INFO(\"Loaded strategy: %s\", strategy_name);\n    return 0;\n}\n\n/**\n * Wildcard                   void *user_context) {\n    (void)payload_len;\n\n    MuseContext *ctx = (MuseContext *)user_context;\n\n    LOG_INFO(\"Received event: %s\", event_type);\n\n    json_error_t jerr;\n json_decref(root);\n        return HC_ERROR_PACK(HC_SUBSYS_COMMON, HC_SEV_ERROR, HC_EC_DEPENDENCY_FAILURE);\n    }\n\n    /* Execute strategy */\n    char *recipe_json = NULL;\n    int rc = {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01GrR8RjoYHd6QQe4Mc5f1ch","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /logs/agent/solution.md"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e3cf5d3b-02fc-44ae-b3ed-24d5bfa80ebe","uuid":"7ba08953-8269-4ac6-b9fc-d23436e56f02","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/logs/agent/solution.md","content":"# Solution: Refactor Event Handling into Shared Event Dispatcher Module\n\n## Overview\n\nThis solution implements a major architectural refactoring by creating a centralized, generic event dispatcher module in the `shared` library. This new module encapsulates the Kafka consumption loop and event routing logic that was previously duplicated across multiple services (`governance_hall`, `mint_factory`, and `muse_observer`).\n\n## Analysis\n\n### Current State (Before Refactoring)\n\nThe three services all implement similar patterns:\n1. **governance_hall/src/event_handler.c** (481 lines): Uses a linked-list of callbacks registered via `event_handler_register()`. The `consumer_loop()` function polls Kafka, parses JSON, validates signatures, and dispatches to registered callbacks by iterating through the linked list.\n\n2. **mint_factory/src/event_handler.c** (431 lines): Uses an enum-based switch statement in `dispatch_event()` to route events. Contains `evh_init()` and `evh_shutdown()` lifecycle functions.\n\n3. **muse_observer/src/event_listener.c** (343 lines): Uses a strategy pattern where a single `MuseActionStrategy` receives all events via `dispatch_event()`.\n\n### Common Patterns Identified\n\nAll three services share:\n- Kafka consumer setup (rd_kafka_conf_set, rd_kafka_new, rd_kafka_subscribe)\n- Main consumption loop with polling\n- JSON parsing with jansson\n- Event type extraction from JSON\n- Handler/callback invocation\n- Graceful shutdown via signal handling\n- Error logging and metrics\n\n### Design Decisions\n\n1. **Hash Map for Handler Registry**: More efficient than linked-list for lookup, especially as the number of handlers grows. Uses open addressing with linear probing for simplicity.\n\n2. **String-based Event Type Keys**: Matches the JSON `\"type\"` field directly, avoiding enum conversion overhead.\n\n3. **Callback Signature**: Uses `(const char* event_type, const char* payload, size_t len, void* user_context)` to support both JSON and potential protobuf payloads.\n\n4. **Thread Safety**: Uses mutex protection for handler registration while the dispatcher is running.\n\n5. **Wildcard Handlers**: Support for handlers that receive all events (like the muse_observer pattern).\n\n---\n\n## New Files to Create\n\n### File 1: `shared/event_dispatcher/hc_event_dispatcher.h`\n\n```c\n/**\n * hc_event_dispatcher.h\n *\n * HoloCanvas - Generic Event Dispatcher Module\n * --------------------------------------------\n * A reusable, generic event dispatcher that centralizes the Kafka consumption\n * loop and event routing logic. This module allows microservices to register\n * handlers for specific event types, abstracting away the boilerplate of\n * Kafka consumer setup, JSON parsing, and event dispatching.\n *\n * Usage:\n *   1. Create a dispatcher with event_dispatcher_create()\n *   2. Register handlers for event types with event_dispatcher_register_handler()\n *   3. Start the event loop with event_dispatcher_run()\n *   4. Clean up with event_dispatcher_destroy()\n *\n * Copyright (c) 2023-2024 The HoloCanvas contributors\n * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0\n */\n\n#ifndef HOLOCANVAS_SHARED_EVENT_DISPATCHER_H\n#define HOLOCANVAS_SHARED_EVENT_DISPATCHER_H\n\n#include <stddef.h>\n#include <stdint.h>\n#include <stdbool.h>\n\n/* Include protobuf-c if available, otherwise use forward declaration */\n#ifdef PROTOBUF_C_H_INCLUDED\n#include <protobuf-c/protobuf-c.h>\n#else\n/* Forward declaration for protobuf message base type */\ntypedef struct ProtobufCMessage ProtobufCMessage;\n#endif\n\n/* Include our common error types */\n#include \"common/errors.h\"\n\n#ifdef __cplusplus\nextern \"C\" {\n#endif\n\n/*===========================================================================*/\n/* Types                                                                     */\n/*===========================================================================*/\n\n/**\n * Event handler function signature.\n *\n * Handlers receive:\n *   - event_type: The string key identifying the event type\n *   - payload: The raw JSON payload string (null-terminated)\n *   - payload_len: Length of the payload string\n *   - user_context: User-provided context data passed during dispatcher creation\n *\n * Returns:\n *   HC_OK on success, or an appropriate error code on failure.\n */\ntypedef hc_error_t (*event_handler_func_t)(const char *event_type,\n                                           const char *payload,\n                                           size_t payload_len,\n                                           void *user_context);\n\n/**\n * Alternative handler signature that receives parsed protobuf message.\n * Only used if protobuf-c is available.\n */\ntypedef hc_error_t (*event_handler_proto_func_t)(const ProtobufCMessage *msg,\n                                                  void *user_context);\n\n/**\n * Opaque event dispatcher context.\n */\ntypedef struct EventDispatcher EventDispatcher;\n\n/**\n * Configuration for the event dispatcher.\n */\ntypedef struct {\n    const char *kafka_brokers;   /**< Comma-separated list of Kafka brokers */\n    const char *topic;           /**< Topic to subscribe to */\n    const char *group_id;        /**< Consumer group ID */\n    int poll_timeout_ms;         /**< Kafka poll timeout (default: 500ms) */\n    int max_handlers;            /**< Maximum number of handlers (default: 64) */\n} EventDispatcherConfig;\n\n/*===========================================================================*/\n/* Public API                                                                */\n/*===========================================================================*/\n\n/**\n * Create a new event dispatcher.\n *\n * @param kafka_brokers Comma-separated list of Kafka broker addresses\n * @param topic         The Kafka topic to subscribe to\n * @param group_id      The consumer group ID\n * @param user_context  User-provided context passed to all handlers\n *\n * @return A new EventDispatcher instance, or NULL on failure\n */\nEventDispatcher *event_dispatcher_create(const char *kafka_brokers,\n                                         const char *topic,\n                                         const char *group_id,\n                                         void *user_context);\n\n/**\n * Create a new event dispatcher with extended configuration.\n *\n * @param config       Configuration structure\n * @param user_context User-provided context passed to all handlers\n *\n * @return A new EventDispatcher instance, or NULL on failure\n */\nEventDispatcher *event_dispatcher_create_ex(const EventDispatcherConfig *config,\n                                            void *user_context);\n\n/**\n * Register an event handler for a specific event type.\n *\n * The event_type_key is matched against the \"type\" field in incoming JSON\n * messages. Multiple handlers can be registered for the same event type;\n * they will be called in registration order.\n *\n * @param dispatcher     The event dispatcher instance\n * @param event_type_key The event type string to match (e.g., \"VOTE_CAST\")\n * @param handler        The handler function to invoke for matching events\n *\n * @return HC_OK on success, error code on failure\n */\nhc_error_t event_dispatcher_register_handler(EventDispatcher *dispatcher,\n                                             const char *event_type_key,\n                                             event_handler_func_t handler);\n\n/**\n * Register a wildcard handler that receives all events.\n *\n * Wildcard handlers are called after type-specific handlers.\n *\n * @param dispatcher The event dispatcher instance\n * @param handler    The handler function to invoke for all events\n *\n * @return HC_OK on success, error code on failure\n */\nhc_error_t event_dispatcher_register_wildcard(EventDispatcher *dispatcher,\n                                              event_handler_func_t handler);\n\n/**\n * Start the event consumption and dispatch loop.\n *\n * This function blocks and continuously polls Kafka for messages, dispatching\n * them to registered handlers. It returns when:\n *   - event_dispatcher_stop() is called from another thread\n *   - A fatal error occurs\n *   - The process receives SIGINT or SIGTERM (if signal handling is enabled)\n *\n * @param dispatcher The event dispatcher instance\n */\nvoid event_dispatcher_run(EventDispatcher *dispatcher);\n\n/**\n * Signal the dispatcher to stop processing events.\n *\n * This is thread-safe and can be called from signal handlers.\n *\n * @param dispatcher The event dispatcher instance\n */\nvoid event_dispatcher_stop(EventDispatcher *dispatcher);\n\n/**\n * Check if the dispatcher is currently running.\n *\n * @param dispatcher The event dispatcher instance\n * @return true if running, false otherwise\n */\nbool event_dispatcher_is_running(const EventDispatcher *dispatcher);\n\n/**\n * Destroy the event dispatcher and free all resources.\n *\n * If the dispatcher is still running, this function will stop it first.\n *\n * @param dispatcher The event dispatcher instance to destroy\n */\nvoid event_dispatcher_destroy(EventDispatcher *dispatcher);\n\n/**\n * Get statistics from the dispatcher.\n */\ntypedef struct {\n    uint64_t events_received;\n    uint64_t events_dispatched;\n    uint64_t events_unhandled;\n    uint64_t parse_errors;\n    uint64_t handler_errors;\n} EventDispatcherStats;\n\n/**\n * Get current statistics from the dispatcher.\n *\n * @param dispatcher The event dispatcher instance\n * @param stats      Output parameter for statistics\n *\n * @return HC_OK on success\n */\nhc_error_t event_dispatcher_get_stats(const EventDispatcher *dispatcher,\n                                      EventDispatcherStats *stats);\n\n/*===========================================================================*/\n/* Convenience Macros                                                        */\n/*===========================================================================*/\n\n/**\n * Install signal handlers for graceful shutdown.\n *\n * This macro sets up SIGINT and SIGTERM handlers that call\n * event_dispatcher_stop() on the given dispatcher.\n */\n#define EVENT_DISPATCHER_INSTALL_SIGNALS(dispatcher) \\\n    event_dispatcher_install_signal_handlers(dispatcher)\n\nvoid event_dispatcher_install_signal_handlers(EventDispatcher *dispatcher);\n\n#ifdef __cplusplus\n}\n#endif\n\n#endif /* HOLOCANVAS_SHARED_EVENT_DISPATCHER_H */\n```\n\n### File 2: `shared/event_dispatcher/hc_event_dispatcher.c`\n\n```c\n/**\n * hc_event_dispatcher.c\n *\n * HoloCanvas - Generic Event Dispatcher Implementation\n * -----------------------------------------------------\n * Implementation of the centralized event dispatcher module.\n *\n * Copyright (c) 2023-2024 The HoloCanvas contributors\n * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0\n */\n\n#define _POSIX_C_SOURCE 200809L\n\n#include \"hc_event_dispatcher.h\"\n\n#include <errno.h>\n#include <pthread.h>\n#include <signal.h>\n#include <stdatomic.h>\n#include <stdio.h>\n#include <stdlib.h>\n#include <string.h>\n#include <time.h>\n\n#include <jansson.h>\n#include <librdkafka/rdkafka.h>\n\n/*===========================================================================*/\n/* Configuration Defaults                                                    */\n/*===========================================================================*/\n\n#define DEFAULT_POLL_TIMEOUT_MS  500\n#define DEFAULT_MAX_HANDLERS     64\n#define HANDLER_MAP_LOAD_FACTOR  0.75\n#define MAX_EVENT_TYPE_LEN       128\n\n/*===========================================================================*/\n/* Logging                                                                   */\n/*===========================================================================*/\n\n#define LOG_PREFIX \"[EventDispatcher] \"\n#define LOG_ERROR(fmt, ...) fprintf(stderr, LOG_PREFIX \"ERROR: \" fmt \"\\n\", ##__VA_ARGS__)\n#define LOG_WARN(fmt, ...)  fprintf(stderr, LOG_PREFIX \"WARN:  \" fmt \"\\n\", ##__VA_ARGS__)\n#define LOG_INFO(fmt, ...)  fprintf(stdout, LOG_PREFIX \"INFO:  \" fmt \"\\n\", ##__VA_ARGS__)\n#define LOG_DEBUG(fmt, ...) fprintf(stdout, LOG_PREFIX \"DEBUG: \" fmt \"\\n\", ##__VA_ARGS__)\n\n/*===========================================================================*/\n/* Internal Data Structures                                                  */\n/*===========================================================================*/\n\n/**\n * Hash map entry for event handlers.\n * Uses open addressing with linear probing.\n */\ntypedef struct {\n    char *event_type;               /**< Event type key (owned) */\n    event_handler_func_t handler;   /**< Handler function */\n    bool occupied;                  /**< Slot occupied flag */\n} HandlerEntry;\n\n/**\n * Linked list node for multiple handlers on same event type.\n */\ntypedef struct HandlerNode {\n    event_handler_func_t handler;\n    struct HandlerNode *next;\n} HandlerNode;\n\n/**\n * Hash map bucket with chaining for collision handling.\n */\ntypedef struct {\n    char *event_type;       /**< Event type key (owned) */\n    HandlerNode *handlers;  /**< Linked list of handlers */\n} HandlerBucket;\n\n/**\n * Internal dispatcher state.\n */\nstruct EventDispatcher {\n    /* Kafka handles */\n    rd_kafka_t *rk;\n    rd_kafka_conf_t *rk_conf;\n    rd_kafka_topic_partition_list_t *sub_topics;\n\n    /* Configuration */\n    char *kafka_brokers;\n    char *topic;\n    char *group_id;\n    int poll_timeout_ms;\n\n    /* Handler registry (hash map with chaining) */\n    HandlerBucket *handler_map;\n    size_t handler_map_size;\n    size_t handler_count;\n\n    /* Wildcard handlers */\n    HandlerNode *wildcard_handlers;\n\n    /* Thread safety */\n    pthread_mutex_t handler_lock;\n\n    /* Runtime state */\n    atomic_bool running;\n    void *user_context;\n\n    /* Statistics */\n    atomic_uint_fast64_t stats_received;\n    atomic_uint_fast64_t stats_dispatched;\n    atomic_uint_fast64_t stats_unhandled;\n    atomic_uint_fast64_t stats_parse_errors;\n    atomic_uint_fast64_t stats_handler_errors;\n};\n\n/* Global dispatcher for signal handling */\nstatic EventDispatcher *g_signal_dispatcher = NULL;\n\n/*===========================================================================*/\n/* Hash Function                                                             */\n/*===========================================================================*/\n\n/**\n * djb2 hash function for strings.\n */\nstatic size_t hash_string(const char *str) {\n    size_t hash = 5381;\n    int c;\n    while ((c = *str++)) {\n        hash = ((hash << 5) + hash) + (size_t)c;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\n/*===========================================================================*/\n/* Handler Map Operations                                                    */\n/*===========================================================================*/\n\nstatic HandlerBucket *handler_map_find(EventDispatcher *self, const char *event_type) {\n    if (!self->handler_map || !event_type) return NULL;\n\n    size_t idx = hash_string(event_type) % self->handler_map_size;\n\n    /* Linear probing */\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < self->handler_map_size; ++i) {\n        size_t probe_idx = (idx + i) % self->handler_map_size;\n        HandlerBucket *bucket = &self->handler_map[probe_idx];\n\n        if (!bucket->event_type) {\n            return NULL; /* Empty slot - not found */\n        }\n\n        if (strcmp(bucket->event_type, event_type) == 0) {\n            return bucket;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return NULL;\n}\n\nstatic hc_error_t handler_map_insert(EventDispatcher *self,\n                                     const char *event_type,\n                                     event_handler_func_t handler) {\n    if (!self->handler_map || !event_type || !handler) {\n        return HC_ERROR_PACK(HC_SUBSYS_COMMON, HC_SEV_ERROR, HC_EC_INVALID_ARGUMENT);\n    }\n\n    /* Check load factor */\n    if ((double)self->handler_count / (double)self->handler_map_size > HANDLER_MAP_LOAD_FACTOR) {\n        LOG_WARN(\"Handler map approaching capacity limit\");\n    }\n\n    /* Find or create bucket */\n    size_t idx = hash_string(event_type) % self->handler_map_size;\n    HandlerBucket *bucket = NULL;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < self->handler_map_size; ++i) {\n        size_t probe_idx = (idx + i) % self->handler_map_size;\n        HandlerBucket *probe = &self->handler_map[probe_idx];\n\n        if (!probe->event_type) {\n            /* Empty slot - use it */\n            probe->event_type = strdup(event_type);\n            if (!probe->event_type) {\n                return HC_ERROR_PACK(HC_SUBSYS_COMMON, HC_SEV_ERROR, HC_EC_OUT_OF_MEMORY);\n            }\n            probe->handlers = NULL;\n            bucket = probe;\n            self->handler_count++;\n            break;\n        }\n\n        if (strcmp(probe->event_type, event_type) == 0) {\n            /* Existing bucket for this event type */\n            bucket = probe;\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (!bucket) {\n        LOG_ERROR(\"Handler map is full\");\n        return HC_ERROR_PACK(HC_SUBSYS_COMMON, HC_SEV_ERROR, HC_EC_OUT_OF_MEMORY);\n    }\n\n    /* Add handler to bucket's linked list */\n    HandlerNode *node = malloc(sizeof(HandlerNode));\n    if (!node) {\n        return HC_ERROR_PACK(HC_SUBSYS_COMMON, HC_SEV_ERROR, HC_EC_OUT_OF_MEMORY);\n    }\n\n    node->handler = handler;\n    node->next = bucket->handlers;\n    bucket->handlers = node;\n\n    return HC_OK;\n}\n\nstatic void handler_map_destroy(EventDispatcher *self) {\n    if (!self->handler_map) return;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < self->handler_map_size; ++i) {\n        HandlerBucket *bucket = &self->handler_map[i];\n\n        if (bucket->event_type) {\n            free(bucket->event_type);\n\n            /* Free handler nodes */\n            HandlerNode *node = bucket->handlers;\n            while (node) {\n                HandlerNode *next = node->next;\n                free(node);\n                node = next;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    free(self->handler_map);\n    self->handler_map = NULL;\n}\n\n/*===========================================================================*/\n/* Event Processing                                                          */\n/*===========================================================================*/\n\nstatic int dispatch_to_handlers(EventDispatcher *self,\n                                const char *event_type,\n                                const char *payload,\n                                size_t payload_len) {\n    int handlers_called = 0;\n    bool had_errors = false;\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&self->handler_lock);\n\n    /* Find handlers for this event type */\n    HandlerBucket *bucket = handler_map_find(self, event_type);\n    if (bucket && bucket->handlers) {\n        for (HandlerNode *node = bucket->handlers; node; node = node->next) {\n            hc_error_t err = node->handler(event_type, payload, payload_len, self->user_context);\n            handlers_called++;\n\n            if (err != HC_OK) {\n                LOG_WARN(\"Handler for '%s' returned error: %s\", event_type, hc_error_str(err));\n                had_errors = true;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /* Call wildcard handlers */\n    for (HandlerNode *node = self->wildcard_handlers; node; node = node->next) {\n        hc_error_t err = node->handler(event_type, payload, payload_len, self->user_context);\n        handlers_called++;\n\n        if (err != HC_OK) {\n            LOG_WARN(\"Wildcard handler returned error: %s\", hc_error_str(err));\n            had_errors = true;\n        }\n    }\n\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&self->handler_lock);\n\n    if (had_errors) {\n        atomic_fetch_add(&self->stats_handler_errors, 1);\n    }\n\n    return handlers_called;\n}\n\nstatic void process_message(EventDispatcher *self,\n                           const void *payload,\n                           size_t len) {\n    atomic_fetch_add(&self->stats_received, 1);\n\n    /* Parse JSON */\n    json_error_t jerr;\n    json_t *root = json_loadb((const char *)payload, len, 0, &jerr);\n    if (!root) {\n        LOG_WARN(\"JSON parse error at line %d: %s\", jerr.line, jerr.text);\n        atomic_fetch_add(&self->stats_parse_errors, 1);\n        return;\n    }\n\n    /* Extract event type */\n    json_t *type_json = json_object_get(root, \"type\");\n    if (!json_is_string(type_json)) {\n        LOG_WARN(\"Event missing 'type' field\");\n        json_decref(root);\n        atomic_fetch_add(&self->stats_parse_errors, 1);\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const char *event_type = json_string_value(type_json);\n\n    /* Extract payload (or use entire message) */\n    json_t *payload_json = json_object_get(root, \"payload\");\n    char *payload_str = NULL;\n    size_t payload_len = 0;\n\n    if (payload_json) {\n        payload_str = json_dumps(payload_json, JSON_COMPACT);\n        payload_len = payload_str ? strlen(payload_str) : 0;\n    } else {\n        /* Use entire message as payload */\n        payload_str = strndup((const char *)payload, len);\n        payload_len = len;\n    }\n\n    if (!payload_str) {\n        LOG_ERROR(\"Failed to extract payload\");\n        json_decref(root);\n        return;\n    }\n\n    /* Dispatch to handlers */\n    int handlers_called = dispatch_to_handlers(self, event_type, payload_str, payload_len);\n\n    if (handlers_called > 0) {\n        atomic_fetch_add(&self->stats_dispatched, 1);\n        LOG_DEBUG(\"Event '%s' dispatched to %d handler(s)\", event_type, handlers_called);\n    } else {\n        atomic_fetch_add(&self->stats_unhandled, 1);\n        LOG_DEBUG(\"No handlers for event type '%s'\", event_type);\n    }\n\n    free(payload_str);\n    json_decref(root);\n}\n\n/*===========================================================================*/\n/* Consumer Loop                                                             */\n/*===========================================================================*/\n\nstatic void consumer_loop(EventDispatcher *self) {\n    LOG_INFO(\"Event dispatcher consumer loop started\");\n\n    while (atomic_load(&self->running)) {\n        rd_kafka_message_t *rkmsg = rd_kafka_consumer_poll(self->rk, self->poll_timeout_ms);\n\n        if (!rkmsg) {\n            continue; /* Timeout - poll again */\n        }\n\n        if (rkmsg->err) {\n            if (rkmsg->err == RD_KAFKA_RESP_ERR__PARTITION_EOF) {\n                /* End of partition - not an error */\n                rd_kafka_message_destroy(rkmsg);\n                continue;\n            }\n            LOG_WARN(\"Kafka error: %s\", rd_kafka_message_errstr(rkmsg));\n            rd_kafka_message_destroy(rkmsg);\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        /* Process the message */\n        process_message(self, rkmsg->payload, rkmsg->len);\n\n        rd_kafka_message_destroy(rkmsg);\n    }\n\n    LOG_INFO(\"Event dispatcher consumer loop stopped\");\n}\n\n/*===========================================================================*/\n/* Signal Handling                                                           */\n/*===========================================================================*/\n\nstatic void signal_handler(int sig) {\n    (void)sig;\n    if (g_signal_dispatcher) {\n        event_dispatcher_stop(g_signal_dispatcher);\n    }\n}\n\nvoid event_dispatcher_install_signal_handlers(EventDispatcher *dispatcher) {\n    g_signal_dispatcher = dispatcher;\n\n    struct sigaction sa = {\n        .sa_handler = signal_handler,\n        .sa_flags = 0\n    };\n    sigemptyset(&sa.sa_mask);\n\n    sigaction(SIGINT, &sa, NULL);\n    sigaction(SIGTERM, &sa, NULL);\n}\n\n/*===========================================================================*/\n/* Public API Implementation                                                 */\n/*===========================================================================*/\n\nEventDispatcher *event_dispatcher_create(const char *kafka_brokers,\n event_dispatcher_destroy(dispatcher);\n        return EXIT_FAILURE;\n    }\n\n    /* Install signal handlers for graceful shutdown */\n    EVENT_DISPATCHER_INSTALL_SIGNALS(dispatcher);\n\n (event_dispatcher_get_stats(dispatcher, &stats) == HC_OK) {\n        LOGI(\"Statistics: received=%\" PRIu64 \" dispatched=%\" PRIu64 \" unhandled=%\" PRIu64 \" parse_errors=%\" PRIu64,\n             stats.events_received, stats.events_dispatched,\n             stats.events_unhandled, stats.parse_errors);\n    }\n\n    event_dispatcher_destroy(dispatcher);\n    LOGI(\"Goodbye\");\n    return EXIT_SUCCESS;\n}\n```\n\n### Modification 3: Refactor `services/governance_hall/src/event_handler.c`\n\nCreate refactored version with individual handlers matching the new signature:\n\n```c\n/**\n * {0};\n\n/* Stub gRPC call */\nstatic int grpc_send_evolution(const char *grpc_target, const char *recipe_json) {\n    (void)grpc_target;\n    LOG_INFO(\"Stub gRPC call: %s\", recipe_json);\n    return 0;\n}\n\n/* Load strategy plugin */\nstatic int load_strategy(const char *plugin_dir, const char *strategy_name) {\n    if (g_plugin_handle && strcmp(g_current_strategy, strategy_name) == 0) {\n        return 0; /* Already loaded */\n    }\n\n    /* Unload previous */\n    if (g_plugin_handle) {\n        dlclose(g_plugin_handle);\n        g_plugin_handle = NULL;\n        g_strategy_run = NULL;\n    }\n\n    /* Load new */\n    char path[512];\n    snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"%s/lib%s.so\", plugin_dir, strategy_name);\n\n    g_plugin_handle = dlopen(path, RTLD_NOW | RTLD_LOCAL);\n    if (!g_plugin_handle) {\n        LOG_ERROR(\"dlopen failed for %s: %s\", strategy_name, dlerror());\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    g_strategy_run = (strategy_fn)dlsym(g_plugin_handle, \"muse_strategy_run\");\n    if (!g_strategy_run) {\n        LOG_ERROR(\"Missing muse_strategy_run in %s\", strategy_name);\n        dlclose(g_plugin_handle);\n        g_plugin_handle = NULL;\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    strncpy(g_current_strategy, strategy_name, sizeof(g_current_strategy) - 1);\n    LOG_INFO(\"Loaded strategy: %s\", strategy_name);\n    return 0;\n}\n\n/**\n * Wildcard handler for all Muse events.\n */\nhc_error_t handle_muse_event(const char *event_type,\n                             const char *payload,\n                             size_t payload_len,\n                             void *user_context) {\n    (void)payload_len;\n\n    MuseContext *ctx = (MuseContext *)user_context;\n\n    LOG_INFO(\"Received event: %s\", event_type);\n\n    json_error_t jerr;\n    json_t *root = json_loads(payload, 0, &jerr);\n    if (!root) {\n        LOG_WARN(\"Invalid JSON: %s\", jerr.text);\n        return HC_ERROR_PACK(HC_SUBSYS_COMMON, HC_SEV_WARNING, HC_EC_DATA_CORRUPT);\n    }\n\n    /* Check for strategy field */\n    const char *strategy = json_string_value(json_object_get(root, \"strategy\"));\n    if (!strategy) {\n        LOG_INFO(\"No strategy specified; ignoring event\");\n        json_decref(root);\n        return HC_OK;\n    }\n\n    /* Load strategy plugin */\n    if (load_strategy(ctx->plugin_dir, strategy) != 0) {\n        json_decref(root);\n        return HC_ERROR_PACK(HC_SUBSYS_COMMON, HC_SEV_ERROR, HC_EC_DEPENDENCY_FAILURE);\n    }\n\n    /* Execute strategy */\n    char *recipe_json = NULL;\n    int rc = g_strategy_run(payload, &recipe_json);\n    if (rc != 0 || !recipe_json) {\n        LOG_ERROR(\"Strategy '%s' failed (rc=%d)\", strategy, rc);\n        json_decref(root);\n        return HC_ERROR_PACK(HC_SUBSYS_COMMON, HC_SEV_ERROR, HC_EC_DEPENDENCY_FAILURE);\n    }\n\n    /* Send to Mint-Factory */\n    rc = grpc_send_evolution(ctx->grpc_target, recipe_json);\n    free(recipe_json);\n\n    json_decref(root);\n    return rc == 0 ? HC_OK : HC_ERROR_PACK(HC_SUBSYS_COMMON, HC_SEV_ERROR, HC_EC_NETWORK_UNREACHABLE);\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Summary of Changes\n\n### New Files Created\n\n| File | Description |\n|------|-------------|\n| `shared/event_dispatcher/hc_event_dispatcher.h` | Header defining the EventDispatcher API |\n| `shared/event_dispatcher/hc_event_dispatcher.c` | Implementation of the event dispatcher |\n\n### Modified Files\n\n| File | Changes |\n|------|---------|\n| `shared/CMakeLists.txt` | Added event_dispatcher sources and jansson dependency |\n| `services/governance_hall/src/main.c` | Replaced consumer loop with EventDispatcher usage |\n| `services/governance_hall/src/event_handler.c` | Removed dispatch logic, kept individual handlers |\n| `services/mint_factory/src/main.c` | Replaced consumer loop with EventDispatcher usage |\n| `services/mint_factory/src/event_handler.c` | Removed dispatch logic, converted handlers |\n| `services/muse_observer/src/main.c` | Replaced event loop with EventDispatcher usage |\n| `services/muse_observer/src/event_listener.c` | Converted to wildcard handler |\n\n### Code Reduction\n\nThe refactoring removes approximately **400-500 lines** of duplicated boilerplate code across the three services, replacing it with simple handler registration calls.\n\n### Key Benefits\n\n1. **Centralized Kafka Management**: All Kafka configuration and connection logic is in one place\n2. **Consistent Error Handling**: Unified logging and statistics across all services\n3. **Easier Testing**: Services can mock the dispatcher or test handlers in isolation\n4. **Simplified Handler Signatures**: All handlers follow the same pattern\n5. **Better Observability**: Built-in statistics for monitoring\n6. **Thread Safety**: Proper mutex protection for handler registration\n7. **Graceful Shutdown**: Signal handling built into the dispatcher\n\n### Build Verification\n\nThe project should compile after these changes with:\n\n```bash\ncd /app/project/build\ncmake .. -DENABLE_TESTS=OFF\nmake -j$(nproc)\n```\n\nEach service's CMakeLists.txt already links against `shared_lib` (HoloCanvas::Shared), so no additional changes are needed to the service build files.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}
